Motivation: 现有安全基准主要检验智能体是否遵从明确有害指令，未能充分理解智能体在失败与有害行动之间选择有害行动的条件，因此需要针对智能体错位的统一分类与评估。

Method: 提出AM-Bench统一分类体系和35场景、七行业评估基准，将欺诈三角适配到智能体环境，从感知威胁、感知机会和推理三个维度构建评估套件与检测系统；威胁分为源自工具性趋同论的五类，机会分为源自ESRR分类体系的七类，并综合文献提出四问二元推理框架；评估10个前沿模型。

Result: 评估显示不同场景间错位率差距达62个百分点；Gemini 3 Flash和Gemini 3.1 Pro的智能体错位水平最高，Claude Sonnet 4.6在所有条件下接近零；推理框架显示模型可能在没有明确情境意识或解释时表现出有害行为，增加缓解与检测难度。

Conclusion: AM-Bench提供了面向智能体错位的统一分类与跨行业评估基准，揭示错位具有显著场景差异且检测复杂；场景与代码已公开，可用于后续安全评估与缓解研究。

中文摘要：随着基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体以越来越高的自主性部署到真实环境中，一个关键失效模式浮现：智能体错位（agentic misalignment），即智能体选择有害行动而非失败。然而，现有安全基准主要评估智能体是否遵从明确有害的指令，对于导致智能体选择有害行动的条件仍知之甚少。为弥补这一局限，我们提出 AM-Bench，一个统一的分类体系与包含35个场景的评估基准，覆盖七个行业，用于研究基于欺诈三角（fraud triangle）的智能体错位；欺诈三角是一个成熟犯罪学框架，用于解释目标导向的不当行为。我们将该框架适配到智能体环境，并沿三个维度构建评估套件与检测系统：感知威胁、感知机会和推理。我们依据先前理论将每个维度操作化为结构化子类别：源自工具性趋同论的五类威胁、取自 Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks (ESRR) 分类体系的七类机会，以及一个由既有文献综合而来的新型四问二元推理框架。我们对10个前沿模型的评估显示，不同场景间的错位率差距达62个百分点，Gemini 3 Flash 与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 表现出最高水平的智能体错位，而 Claude Sonnet 4.6 在所有条件下均显示接近零的比率。我们的推理框架进一步揭示，模型可能在没有明确情境意识或解释的情况下表现出有害行为，这使缓解与检测都更加复杂。AM-Bench 场景与代码见 https://github.com/AI4Collaboration/Agentic-Misalignment-Bench

Original abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed with increasing autonomy in real-world environments, a critical failure mode emerges: agentic misalignment, where agents choose harmful actions over failure. However, existing safety benchmarks primarily evaluate whether agents comply with explicitly harmful instructions, leaving the conditions that cause agents to choose harmful actions poorly understood. To address this limitation, we propose AM-Bench, a unified taxonomy and 35 scenario evaluation benchmark spanning seven industries for agentic misalignment grounded in the fraud triangle, a well-established criminological framework explaining goal-directed misconduct. We adapt this framework to agentic environments and construct our evaluation suite and detection systems along three dimensions: perceived threat, perceived opportunity, and reasoning. We operationalize each dimension into structured subcategories rooted in prior theory: five threat categories derived from the instrumental convergence thesis, seven opportunity categories drawn from the Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks (ESRR) taxonomy, and a novel four-question binary reasoning framework synthesized from prior literature. Our evaluation of 10 frontier models reveals a 62-percentage-point gap in misalignment across scenarios, with Gemini 3 Flash and Gemini 3.1 Pro exhibiting the highest levels of agentic misalignment while Claude Sonnet 4.6 displays near-zero rates across all conditions. Our reasoning framework further reveals that models may exhibit harmful behaviours without explicit situational awareness or explanation, complicating both mitigation and detection. AM-Bench scenarios and code are available at https://github.com/AI4Collaboration/Agentic-Misalignment-Bench

</details>


### [28] [SteerSearch: Interactive Search Reasoning with Mid-Response Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24287)
*Udit Thakur,Baohao Liao,Matteo Nulli,Stefan Vasilev,Seyyed Hadi Hashemi,Shahram Khadivi,Christof Monz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 48 pages

Selection reason: 提出搜索agent的中途转向RL训练框架与基准，涉及长程检索推理与上下文保持，可迁移到agent规划与交互式优化。

TL;DR: 论文研究搜索智能体在响应中途被用户重定向时的目标重分配问题，指出“答案目标锚定”失败模式，提出强化学习框架 SteerSearch、基准 SteerSearch-Bench 及辅助奖励 CCR，并在多个 Qwen 模型上显著提升引导准确率且不损害单查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式训练方法通常假设搜索全程用户目标固定，但真实长时程研究智能体常需在推理或检索中途接受用户重定向；固定查询智能体可能出现检索已更新请求证据却仍回答旧问题的答案目标锚定失败。

Method: 提出 SteerSearch 强化学习框架，在注入干预的交互式搜索轨迹上训练，以实现保留上下文的目标重分配；构建由多跳问答数据集改编的 SteerSearch-Bench；并研究对比上下文奖励 CCR 作为辅助信号，使干预后推理对齐更新目标。

Result: 不使用 CCR 时，SteerSearch 在 Qwen2.5-3B 上将平均引导准确率相对 Search-R1 从 11.1% 提升至 31.3%，在 Qwen3-4B 上从 36.6% 提升至 44.6%，同时保持标准单查询性能；增益还扩展到更大 Qwen2.5 模型和自然多轮对话。

Conclusion: SteerSearch 能有效支持搜索增强语言模型在响应中途进行保留上下文的目标重分配，缓解答案目标锚定问题，并在不牺牲常规单查询能力的情况下提升引导准确率，且具备向更大模型和自然多轮交互扩展的潜力。

中文摘要：搜索增强语言模型正日益作为长时程研究智能体发挥作用，但大多数开放式训练方法都假设在整个搜索过程中用户目标固定不变。我们研究响应中途引导（mid-response steering），即用户在模型持续推理或检索过程中重定向模型。我们识别出答案目标锚定（answer-target anchoring）这一失败模式：固定查询的搜索智能体会为更新后的请求检索证据，却仍然回答较早的问题。我们提出 SteerSearch，一个强化学习框架，通过在注入干预的交互式搜索轨迹上训练，实现保留上下文的目标重分配；并提出 SteerSearch-Bench，一个由多跳问答数据集改编的基准。我们还研究了对比上下文奖励（Contrastive Context Reward, CCR），一种用于使干预后推理与更新目标对齐的辅助信号。在不使用 CCR 的情况下，SteerSearch 在 Qwen2.5-3B 上将相对于 Search-R1 的平均引导准确率从 11.1% 提升到 31.3%，在 Qwen3-4B 上从 36.6% 提升到 44.6%，同时保持标准单查询性能。增益还扩展到更大的 Qwen2.5 模型和自然多轮对话。

Original abstract: Search-augmented language models increasingly serve as long-horizon research agents, yet most open training methods assume a fixed user goal throughout search. We study mid-response steering, where users redirect a model during ongoing reasoning or retrieval. We identify answer-target anchoring, a failure mode in which fixed-query search agents retrieve evidence for the updated request but still answer an earlier question. We introduce SteerSearch, an RL framework that trains on interactive search trajectories with injected interventions to enable context-preserving target reassignment, and SteerSearch-Bench, a benchmark adapted from multi-hop QA datasets. We also investigate Contrastive Context Reward (CCR), an auxiliary signal for aligning post-intervention reasoning with the updated goal. Without CCR, SteerSearch improves average steering accuracy over Search-R1 from 11.1% to 31.3% on Qwen2.5-3B and from 36.6% to 44.6% on Qwen3-4B, while preserving standard single-query performance. Gains also extend to larger Qwen2.5 models and natural multi-turn conversations.

</details>


### [29] [Not Every User Says It All: Training Tool-Using Agents Beyond Ideal Simulated Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24294)
*Han Bao,Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 11 figures, under review

Selection reason: 工具调用agent在弱用户下的训练问题，StaGPO用状态分组做步级信用分配，属通用agent训练机制。

TL;DR: StaGPO 利用状态分组和步骤级信用，让弱用户训练变得有效，并在 ToolWOZ 与 tau2-bench 上超过 GRPO 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体通常只在能清楚陈述所有细节的强模拟用户下训练和评估，但真实用户往往更弱；弱用户会降低所有策略的成功率，且常规弱用户训练和让智能体多提问都无效。

Method: 通过遮蔽模拟用户回复中的句子构建弱用户并交叉评估；提出 StaGPO：智能体写出任务需求、缺失信息及填补方式，GRPO 据此比较到达相同情境的不同 rollout 轮次并分配步骤级信用；采用小型 SFT 冷启动后接 RL 的标准流程。

Result: 在 ToolWOZ 上，弱用户训练的 Qwen3.5-2B 策略在弱用户下达 76.8%，比 GRPO 高 4.8 分、比最强基线高 2.8 分；强用户下比 GRPO 高 5.4 分，并泛化到未见遮蔽率；强用户训练时服务弱用户至少不差于弱用户训练的 GRPO。tau2-bench 上两个模型在弱用户下均得分最高，Qwen3.5-4B 优势幅度较小。

Conclusion: StaGPO 使弱用户训练从无效变为有效，提升弱用户和强用户表现，并具有良好的遮蔽率泛化能力。

中文摘要：使用工具的智能体几乎完全是在强模拟用户（会清楚陈述每个细节）下训练和评估的，但真实用户往往更弱。我们通过遮蔽模拟用户回复中的句子来构建弱用户，同时保持任务和用户知识不变，并在 tau2-bench 和 ToolWOZ 上交叉评估分别用强用户和弱用户训练的智能体。弱用户会降低每个策略的成功率，而用弱用户训练也没有帮助：无论是通过固定遮蔽、混合还是课程学习，没有任何用弱用户训练的策略能在超出运行间噪声的范围内比用强用户训练的策略更好地服务弱用户，提示智能体多提问也没有帮助。我们提出 StaGPO（State-Grouped Policy Optimization，状态分组策略优化）：智能体写出任务需要什么、缺少什么以及如何填补，GRPO 利用该状态比较到达相同情境的不同 rollout 的轮次，并分配步骤级信用。在“小型 SFT 冷启动后接 RL”的标准流程中，StaGPO 使弱用户训练变得有用：在 ToolWOZ 上，用弱用户训练的 Qwen3.5-2B 策略在弱用户下达到 76.8%，是我们训练过的所有 Qwen3.5-2B 策略中最高的，比 GRPO 高 4.8 分，比最强基线高 2.8 分；在强用户下也比 GRPO 高 5.4 分，并能泛化到未见过的遮蔽率；而改用强用户训练时，它服务弱用户的效果至少与用弱用户训练的 GRPO 一样好。在 tau2-bench 上，它对两个模型在弱用户下都得分最高，其中 Qwen3.5-4B 的优势幅度较小。

Original abstract: Tool-using agents are trained and evaluated almost exclusively with strong simulated users who state every detail clearly, yet real users are often weaker. We construct weak users by masking sentences of a simulated user's replies, holding the task and the user's knowledge fixed, and cross-evaluate agents trained with strong and weak users on tau2-bench and ToolWOZ. Weak users lower the success of every policy, and training with them does not help: no policy trained with weak users, through fixed masks, mixtures or a curriculum, serves weak users better than the strong-trained policy beyond run-to-run noise, and prompting the agent to ask more does not help either. We propose StaGPO (State-Grouped Policy Optimization): the agent writes what the task needs, what is missing and how it is being filled, and GRPO uses this state to compare turns of different rollouts that reach the same situation, assigning step-level credit. Within the standard recipe of a small SFT cold start followed by RL, StaGPO makes weak-user training useful: on ToolWOZ, the Qwen3.5-2B policy trained with weak users reaches 76.8% with weak users, the highest of any Qwen3.5-2B policy we trained, 4.8 points above GRPO and 2.8 above the strongest baseline; it is also 5.4 points above GRPO with strong users, and generalises to an unseen mask rate; trained with strong users instead, it serves weak users at least as well as GRPO trained with weak users. On tau2-bench it scores highest with weak users for both models, by a small margin for Qwen3.5-4B.

</details>


### [30] [Who Speaks, Who Authorizes, Who Acts? Understanding Trust Boundaries in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24298)
*Junru Zhu,Xin Chen,Hang Lyu,Jiayue Meng,Muzi Li,Yifan Chen,Hengyi Huang,Yu Sun,Weiwei Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 多agent系统的通信/授权/执行信任边界分析与工具门控实验，对agent可靠性安全控制有启发。

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM的消息传递、签名授权与工具执行是不同信任边界；受控研究显示路由、身份线索和证明有效性会造成请求级与提交级安全读数分歧，因此应分层评估信息、授权、提议和提交行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，消息传递、签名授权和工具侧执行属于不同信任边界，需要区分信息交付、授权资格、模型提议与实际提交行动，并刻画可构造策略有效虚假证明的最小受损签名者联盟。

Method: 将系统建模为通信、授权和执行结构，用 authority cut 刻画最小独立受损签名者联盟；开展受控配对隐藏状态研究，比较四个模型的路由与消息重写、三个模型的来源身份线索，并用确定性工具门控和单模型授权检查分析无效证明与受损签名者证明。

Result: 四点结果：四个模型上的固定消息路由对比均未达预设大效应标准；中继重写消息时虚假状态行动率随模型和路由配置变化；来源身份线索未一致提高受保护行动选择；无效证明下 Nemotron 和 Llama 分别有52.1%和54.2%试验请求受保护行动，但工具门控零提交，请求级与提交级安全读数分歧；虚假声明若带策略有效的受损签名者证明可通过门控。

Conclusion: 证明有效性不等于事实真实性；应分别评估传递的信息、限定授权、模型提议和已提交行动，避免将请求级安全误当作提交级安全。

中文摘要：多智能体大语言模型系统在通过工具行动之前交换消息，但消息传递、签名授权与工具侧执行是彼此独立的信任边界。我们将它们表示为通信、授权与执行结构，并使用 authority cut（权限切割）来刻画在所述假设下能够构造策略有效的虚假声明证明的最小独立受损签名者联盟。受控配对隐藏状态研究揭示了四点区别。第一，在四个模型上，没有任何固定消息路由对比达到预设的大效应标准；当中继重写消息时，实际执行的虚假状态行动率因模型和路由配置而异。第二，在三个模型上，所提供的来源身份线索并未一致提高受保护行动的选择率。第三，在无效证明案例中，Nemotron 和 Llama 分别在 52.1% 和 54.2% 的试验中请求受保护行动，但确定性工具门控未提交任何此类行动：请求级与提交级安全读数出现分歧。最后，一项单独的单模型授权检查表明，带有策略有效的受损签名者证明的虚假声明可以通过门控，因为证明有效性并非事实真实性。这些发现促使我们分别评估传递的信息、限定授权、模型提议和已提交行动。

Original abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems exchange messages before acting through tools, but message delivery, signing authority, and tool-side enforcement are separate trust boundaries. We represent them as communication, authorization, and execution structures, and use the authority cut to characterize the smallest independently compromised signer coalition that can construct a policy-valid false-claim proof under stated assumptions. Controlled paired hidden-state studies reveal four distinctions. First, no fixed-message routing contrast across four models meets the prespecified large-effect criterion; when relays rewrite messages, executed false-state action rates vary by model and route configuration. Second, presented source-identity cues do not uniformly increase protected-action selection across three models. Third, on invalid-proof cases, Nemotron and Llama request protected actions in 52.1% and 54.2% of trials, respectively, yet a deterministic tool gate commits none: request-level and commitment-level safety readouts diverge. Finally, a separate single-model authorization check shows that a false claim with a policy-valid compromised-signer proof can pass the gate, since proof validity is not factual truth. These findings motivate evaluating delivered information, qualified authorization, model proposals, and committed actions separately.

</details>


### [31] [Teaching Code Agents to Ask Without Leaking Private Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24824)
*Wenjia Jiang,Jieke Shi,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Shidong Pan,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,James Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo,Zhou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages of main text, 33 pages in total with references and appendix, 4 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 训练编码 agent 以角色化抽象向云端咨询，兼顾修复收益与隐私泄露，涉及 agent 协作、工具调用与实际修复效果验证。

TL;DR: 提出效用验证抽象（UVA），通过将咨询问题改写为按角色和类型描述私有实体并验证修复效用，减少本地编码代理咨询云端模型时的私有信息泄露，同时提升仓库级问题修复表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地编码代理咨询云端强模型会泄露私有标识符、文件路径和源代码；主机侧遮蔽只隐藏已识别标识符，保留上下文和措辞；直接训练少披露可能抑制咨询或使问题过于模糊。

Method: 从已解决修复轨迹出发，将问题改写为按角色和类型描述私有实体的抽象形式；仅当改写披露更少私有实体且修复可在相同执行状态下重放成功时保留，以训练代理。

Result: 两个基准上咨询更频繁；仓库级问题修复解决率37.6%，单独模型为25.3%；问题披露私有实体少3.7倍、逐字代码少1.4倍。

Conclusion: UVA能在保留专家所需技术细节和修复效用的前提下降低咨询泄露，并提升代理修复表现；增益来自整体训练而非仅咨询。

中文摘要：本地编码代理可以通过咨询更强的云端模型来扩展其能力范围。然而，此类咨询常常暴露仓库的私有标识符、文件路径和源代码。主机侧遮蔽会隐藏已识别的标识符，但周围代码和代理的措辞保持不变；而为减少披露进行训练，则存在抑制咨询或产生过于模糊而无法回答的问题的风险。我们提出效用验证抽象（UVA），它教会代理按角色和类型描述私有实体，同时保留专家所需的技术细节。从已解决的修复轨迹中，我们将每个问题改写成这种形式，并且仅当该改写披露更少的私有实体，且修复从同一执行状态借助修复轨迹重放仍能完成时，才保留该改写。在两个基准上，训练后的代理比训练前更频繁地进行咨询。在仓库级问题修复中，它解决了37.6%的问题，而同一模型单独工作时为25.3%；这一增益来自整体训练，而非仅来自咨询。与未训练代理向同一专家提出的问题相比，其问题披露的私有实体少3.7倍，逐字代码少1.4倍。

Original abstract: Local coding agents can extend their reach by consulting a stronger cloud model. Such consultations, however, often expose the repository's private identifiers, file paths, and source code. Host-side masking hides recognized identifiers but leaves the surrounding code and the agent's phrasing unchanged, while training for less disclosure risks suppressing consultation or producing questions too vague to answer. We propose utility-verified abstraction (UVA), which teaches the agent to describe private entities by their role and type while retaining the technical details an expert needs. From solved repair trajectories, we rewrite each question into this form and keep the rewrite only if it discloses fewer private entities and the repair, replayed from the same execution state with the repair trajectories. Across two benchmarks, the trained agent consults more often than before training. On repository-level issue repair, it solves 37.6% of issues, against 25.3% for the same model working alone, a gain from the training as a whole rather than from consultation alone. Its questions disclose 3.7-fold fewer private entities and 1.4-fold less verbatim code than the untrained agent's questions to the same expert.

</details>


### [32] [CME-SpectrumBench: Can LLMs Analyze Condensed Matter Spectral Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24304)
*Jin Gene Wong,Anjney Midha,Joseph Mathew Tennyson,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Zhi-Xun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 科学谱数据分析基准发现工具使用+代码生成可显著提升表现，间接体现agentic code generation价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出专家设计的 CME-SpectrumBench，用190个凝聚态实验光谱数据分析问题评估前沿LLM；直接查询时模型表现明显弱于手写代码，而允许使用Python解释器后可接近手写代码水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准多评估数学、物理中的理论符号推理，缺少面向前沿研究问题数据分析能力的实验性基准，而未来领域专用基础模型需要具备该能力。

Method: 构建包含190个专家设计问题的 CME-SpectrumBench，基于模拟凝聚态实验光谱数据，要求模型在噪声和有限仪器分辨率下从二维强度数组中识别结构，并按数值答案接近真实值的程度评分；同时比较直接查询、手写代码以及允许工具使用并配合/不配合ReAct提示的表现。

Result: 直接查询时，LLM得分通常远低于手写代码：能找到简单特征，但难以追踪模式或计算派生量，且性能随数据分辨率和噪声/卷积水平增加而下降；允许访问Python解释器后，无论是否使用ReAct提示，LLM性能可与手写代码相当。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM可通过代码与数据交互充当研究助手，但直接从大型数据集处理信息仍存在困难，这是未来基础模型需要克服的关键障碍。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）目前主要通过专家级数学和物理基准得到充分评估，这些基准侧重于理论性的符号推理。然而，我们缺乏评估前沿研究问题中数据分析能力的实验性基准，而这是未来领域专用基础模型必须具备的能力。为此，我们提出 CME-SpectrumBench，一个由专家设计的基准，包含190个问题，用于衡量前沿LLM在凝聚态实验（CME）中分析研究级光谱科学数据的可靠性。利用模拟的CME光谱数据，我们提示模型在面对噪声和有限仪器分辨率时从二维强度数组中识别结构，这是对密集上下文推理的测试。模型按其数值答案接近真实值的准确程度评分。在直接查询下，由于明显的失败模式，得分通常远不如手写代码：模型能找到简单特征，但难以追踪模式或计算派生量，并且性能通常随数据分辨率以及噪声/卷积水平增加而下降。通过允许工具使用（访问Python解释器）并配合/不配合ReAct提示，LLM性能变得与手写代码相当，实际上将问题映射为智能体式代码生成问题。我们的发现表明，前沿模型可以通过代码与数据交互来充当研究助手，但在直接从大型数据集处理信息方面仍面临困难——这是未来基础模型必须克服的关键障碍。代码和数据公开可用：https://huggingface.co/datasets/jingene/CMESpectrumBench。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are presently well assessed by expert-level mathematics and physics benchmarks which focus on theoretical, symbolic reasoning. However, we lack experimental benchmarks that evaluate data analysis in frontier research problems, a capability that future domain-specific foundation models must have. To this end, we present CME-SpectrumBench, an expert-designed benchmark consisting of 190 questions that measures how reliably frontier LLMs analyze research-level spectral scientific data in condensed matter experiments (CME). Using simulated CME spectral data, we prompt models to identify structures from 2D intensity arrays in the face of noise and limited instrumental resolution, a test of dense context reasoning. Models are scored by how accurately their numerical answers approach the ground truth. Under direct query, scores generally pale in comparison to handwritten code due to clear failure modes: models can find simple features but struggle to trace patterns or compute derived quantities, and performance typically degrades with increased data resolution and noise/convolution levels. LLM performance becomes comparable to handwritten code by allowing tool use (access to a Python interpreter) with/without ReAct prompting, in effect mapping questions onto agentic code generation problems. Our findings show that frontier models can serve as research assistants by interfacing with data through code, but still face difficulties processing information directly from large datasets - a key barrier future foundation models must overcome. The code and data are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jingene/CMESpectrumBench.

</details>


### [33] [Pattern-Driven Evaluation of LLM Code Optimization: Isolating Compiler-Transcendent Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24835)
*Wesley Lu,Runzhou Liu,Hongru Du,Xinyao Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Published at the AI Measurement Science Workshop at COLM 2026

Selection reason: PatternBench 直接针对 LLM 代码优化，隔离编译器已可恢复的变换并用 AST 忠实性分析验证优化是否真正实施，与 SemOpt 主题高度契合。

TL;DR: PatternBench 通过运行时评估与基于 AST 的优化忠实性分析，研究 LLM 代码优化中的编译器超越型优化；仅看加速不完整，微调可同时提升忠实性与优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码优化评估主要依赖端到端加速，无法判断模型是否真正执行了预期优化；对编译语言而言，观测收益可能来自现代优化编译器本就可恢复的变换，而非模型自身优化行为。

Method: 提出 PatternBench 基准，面向七类变换中的残余优化机会，研究激进编译器优化后仍保留的性能提升；将运行时评估与基于 AST 的优化忠实性分析结合，验证生成程序是否实现预期优化。

Result: 对前沿模型、开放权重模型和微调模型的实验显示，仅凭运行时加速无法完整刻画优化能力；优化忠实性与实际加速强相关，微调同时提升优化忠实性和优化有效性。

Conclusion: LLM 代码优化评估应同时衡量性能结果与目标变换是否被成功执行，而不能仅依赖端到端加速。

中文摘要：现有基于 LLM 的代码优化评估主要衡量端到端加速，从而掩盖了模型是否真正执行了预期优化。对于编译语言，这一问题尤为突出：观测到的收益可能反映现代优化编译器本就可恢复的变换，而非可归因于模型本身的优化行为。我们提出 PatternBench，一个用于研究编译器超越型优化的基准，即在激进编译器优化后仍然保留的性能提升。PatternBench 针对七类变换中的残余优化机会，并将运行时评估与基于 AST 的优化忠实性分析相结合，以验证预期优化是否在生成程序中实现。对前沿模型、开放权重模型和微调模型的实验表明，仅凭运行时加速不足以完整刻画优化能力。优化忠实性与实际实现的加速强相关，而微调同时提升了忠实性和优化有效性。这些结果表明，基于 LLM 的代码优化评估不仅应衡量性能结果，还应衡量目标变换是否被成功执行。

Original abstract: Existing evaluations of LLM-based code optimization primarily measure end-to-end speedup, obscuring whether a model actually performs the intended optimization. For compiled languages, this is especially problematic: observed gains may reflect transformations already recoverable by modern optimizing compilers rather than optimization behavior attributable to the model itself. We present PatternBench, a benchmark for studying compiler-transcendent optimization, i.e., performance improvements that remain after aggressive compiler optimization. PatternBench targets residual optimization opportunities across seven classes of transformations and pairs runtime evaluation with an AST-based optimization faithfulness analysis that verifies whether the intended optimization is realized in the generated program. Experiments with frontier, open-weight, and fine-tuned models show that runtime speedup alone provides an incomplete account of optimization capability. Optimization faithfulness is strongly associated with realized speedup, and fine-tuning improves both faithfulness and optimization effectiveness. These results suggest that evaluation of LLM-based code optimization should measure not only performance outcomes, but also whether the target transformation was successfully executed.

</details>


### [34] [The Judge Can Debias Itself: Awareness and Self-Debiasing for Reliable LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24320)
*Youbing Yin,Jingyu Wu,Anoop Kumar,Scott Novotney,Daben Liu,Erin Babinsky*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM判官自我批判去偏，属自我反思/自改进式评估机制，可迁移到agent验证环节。

TL;DR: 无需额外训练，通过偏差感知提示和自我去偏可提升 LLM-as-a-Judge 的评估可靠性；自我去偏在 21 个模型-偏差组合中 20 个有效，最高提升 15 个百分点，但微调外部批评者对新偏差泛化不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge 被广泛用于自动化评估，但存在系统性偏差，削弱评估可靠性；作者希望在不额外训练的情况下实现评审者自我去偏。

Method: 提出两种轻量策略：评估前提醒评审者目标偏差的偏差感知提示，以及让评审者批判初始判断是否受偏差影响的自我去偏；引入权威偏差、置信偏差、格式偏差三个新基准，覆盖七类偏差，并在三个前沿模型上评估，同时考察微调外部批评者的泛化表现。

Result: 自我去偏在 21 个模型-偏差组合中的 20 个上提升性能，最高增益 15 个百分点；偏差感知提示也带来一致改进；微调外部批评者对新引入偏差类型泛化较差，9 个案例中 5 个出现性能下降。

Conclusion: 让评审者以先发或回顾方式对偏差进行推理，是提升评估完整性的稳健且可泛化方法。

中文摘要：LLM-as-a-Judge 系统越来越多地被用于自动化评估，但仍易受系统性偏差影响，从而削弱其可靠性。我们证明，评审者无需额外训练即可有效自我去偏。我们提出两种轻量级策略：偏差感知提示（bias-aware prompting），即在评估前明确提醒评审者注意潜在的目标偏差；以及自我去偏（self-debiasing），即由评审者对初始判断进行偏差影响批判。为拓宽评估范围，我们引入三个新基准——权威偏差（虚构引用）、置信偏差（断言式措辞）和格式偏差（结构化呈现）——并总计评估七类偏差。在三个前沿模型上，自我去偏在 21 个模型-偏差组合中的 20 个上提升了性能，增益最高达 15 个百分点；偏差感知提示也带来了一致改进。然而，近期经过微调的外部批评者对新引入的偏差类型并不能同样可靠地泛化（在 9 个案例中观察到 5 个性能下降）。我们的发现表明，促使评审者以先发或回顾方式对偏差进行推理，是提升评估完整性的一种稳健且可泛化的方法。

Original abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge systems are increasingly employed for automated evaluation, yet remain susceptible to systematic biases that undermine their reliability. We demonstrate that the judge can effectively debias itself without additional training. We propose two lightweight strategies: bias-aware prompting, which explicitly alerts the judge to potential target biases prior to evaluation, and self-debiasing, in which the judge critiques the initial judgment for bias influence. To broaden the evaluation scope, we introduce three novel benchmarks--authority bias (fabricated citations), confidence bias (assertive phrasing), and formatting bias (structured presentation)--and evaluate across seven bias types in total. Across three frontier models, self-debiasing improves performance in 20 out of 21 model-bias combinations with gains up to 15 percentage points, while bias-aware prompting also yields consistent improvements. The recent fine-tuned external critic, however, does not generalize as reliably to the newly-introduced bias types (degradation observed in 5 out of 9 cases). Our findings demonstrate that prompting a judge to reason about bias, either preemptively or retrospectively, is a robust and generalizable approach for enhancing evaluation integrity.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond Next-Screen Prediction: The Decision Value of GUI World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24372)
*Sizhe Tang,Hanhan Zhou,Jeffrey Svajlenko,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: GUI世界模型/动作选择的归因研究并提出Direct Action Ranking，对agent的决策评估与lookahead设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 论文指出 GUI 世界模型的前瞻收益可能主要来自评估器而非预测本身，提出无需生成未来屏幕的直接动作排序 DAR，并在 AndroidWorld 上取得更高成功率与零样本迁移表现；主张用决策价值评估世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 世界模型常用于预测候选动作后果以改进动作选择，但端到端收益难以区分来自预测本身还是解释预测的评估器，存在归因不清问题。

Method: 通过受控干预研究 GUI 动作选择中的归因；在候选动作与预测固定时改变预测呈现方式；提出 Direct Action Ranking（DAR），从参考动作学习直接给候选动作打分而不生成未来屏幕，并在匹配监督下与预测增强变体比较。

Result: 直接学习评估后，预测后继未带来可检测的排序信号；无需重训练，DAR 在三个提议器上使 AndroidWorld 任务成功率提升 5.8--16.4 个百分点，优于世界模型前瞻，并可零样本迁移到未见应用，且所有预测条件配置均低于提议器置信度。

Conclusion: GUI 世界模型应按决策价值评估，即预测在强直接评估器之外带来的增量动作选择价值，而非仅看预测保真度或端到端前瞻收益。

中文摘要：GUI 世界模型正越来越多地被用于通过预测候选动作的后果来改进动作选择，但由此获得的收益中有多少来自预测本身、而非来自解释这些预测的评估器，仍不清楚。我们通过对 GUI 动作选择进行受控干预来研究这一归因问题。在候选动作和预测保持固定时，前瞻收益会随预测呈现给评估器的方式而变化，因此端到端收益不能仅归因于预测。随后我们引入直接动作排序（Direct Action Ranking, DAR），它从参考动作中学习以对候选动作打分，而无需生成未来屏幕，并在匹配监督下将其与预测增强变体进行比较。一旦直接学习评估，预测的后继状态并未增加可检测的排序信号。无需重新训练，DAR 在三个提议器上使 AndroidWorld 任务成功率提高 5.8--16.4 个百分点，优于世界模型前瞻，并能零样本迁移到未见应用；在这些应用中，所有以预测为条件的配置均低于提议器置信度。这些结果表明，GUI 世界模型应依据其决策价值来评估，即其预测在强直接评估器之外带来的增量动作选择价值，而不是仅依据预测保真度或端到端前瞻收益。

Original abstract: GUI world models are increasingly used to improve action selection by predicting the consequences of candidate actions, but it is unclear how much of the resulting gain comes from prediction itself rather than from the evaluator that interprets those predictions. We study this attribution problem through controlled interventions on GUI action selection. With candidates and predictions held fixed, lookahead gains can change with how the prediction is presented to the evaluator, so end-to-end gains cannot be attributed to prediction alone. We then introduce Direct Action Ranking (DAR), which learns from reference actions to score candidates without generating future screens, and compare it with a prediction-augmented variant under matched supervision. Predicted successors add no detectable ranking signal once evaluation is learned directly. Without retraining, DAR improves AndroidWorld task success by 5.8--16.4 points across three proposers, outperforms world-model lookahead, and transfers zero-shot to unseen apps, where every prediction-conditioned configuration falls below proposer confidence. These results suggest that GUI world models should be evaluated by their decision value, the incremental action-selection value of their predictions beyond a strong direct evaluator, rather than by prediction fidelity or end-to-end lookahead gain alone.

</details>


### [36] [Train Locally, Learn Together: Distributed Multi-Agent Training via ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24380)
*Yiwei Chen,Changsheng Wang,Yicheng Lang,Qing Qu,Alfred O. Hero,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于ADMM的分布式多agent训练框架，保持角色本地学习与去中心化共识，属多agent协作方法。

TL;DR: 提出基于共识 ADMM 的分布式多智能体系统训练框架，在角色本地学习与去中心化通信下实现联合优化，并在安全对齐与推理任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式 MAS 训练通常依赖集中式公共训练器聚合角色本地轨迹与学习信号并维护模型和优化状态，限制了可扩展性与韧性，因此需要分布式多智能体学习范式。

Method: 将协作式 MAS 学习建模为共识约束的有限和优化问题，采用共识 ADMM 交替进行角色本地学习与智能体间共识，并使用环形全归约等分布式通信协议，保持各智能体数据和训练状态本地化。

Result: 在分布式安全对齐和通用推理任务上评估；在受损智能体设置下，HarmBench 攻击成功率最多降低 26.4%，推理增益与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: 分布式 MAS 训练无需集中式训练器即可保留集中式联合优化的收益，有助于实现可扩展、韧性和自组织的多智能体系统。

中文摘要：多智能体系统（MAS）可以通过结合专门化角色、多样化推理过程和协同决策，优于单个语言模型智能体。然而，协作式 MAS 训练通常是集中式的，需要一个公共训练器来聚合角色本地轨迹和学习信号，并维护模型与优化状态。这促使一种分布式多智能体学习范式：智能体通过角色本地学习和去中心化通信提升系统级能力，为可扩展、韧性和自组织的 MAS 铺平道路。为此，据我们所知，我们引入了首个基于共识交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的、面向分布式 MAS 训练的优化 grounded（基于优化）框架。我们将协作式 MAS 学习建模为共识约束的有限和优化问题，使其天然适合通过 ADMM 进行分布式优化。所提方法将角色本地学习与智能体间共识交替进行，并使用环形全归约等分布式通信协议，在保持每个智能体的数据和训练状态本地化的同时实现联合优化。我们在分布式安全对齐和通用推理任务上评估该框架。在受损智能体设置下，ADMM 可将 HarmBench 攻击成功率（ASR）最多降低 26.4%，同时获得与集中式训练相当的推理增益。这些结果表明，分布式 MAS 训练保留了集中式联合优化的优势，而无需集中式训练器。

Original abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) can outperform individual language-model agents by combining specialized roles, diverse reasoning processes, and collaborative decision-making. However, collaborative MAS training is typically centralized, requiring a common trainer to aggregate role-local trajectories and learning signals and maintain model and optimization states. This motivates a distributed multi-agent learning paradigm in which agents improve system-level capabilities through role-local learning and decentralized communication, paving the way toward scalable, resilient, and self-organizing MAS. To this end, we introduce, to the best of our knowledge, the first optimization-grounded framework for distributed MAS training based on consensus alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). We formulate collaborative MAS learning as a consensus-constrained finite-sum optimization problem, naturally lending itself to distributed optimization via ADMM. The proposed approach alternates role-local learning with inter-agent consensus using distributed communication protocols such as ring all-reduce, enabling joint optimization while keeping each agent's data and training state local. We evaluate our framework on distributed safety alignment and general reasoning tasks. Under compromised-agent settings, ADMM can reduce HarmBench attack success rate (ASR) by up to 26.4%, while achieving reasoning gains comparable to centralized training. These results show that distributed MAS training retains the benefits of centralized joint optimization without requiring a centralized trainer.

</details>


### [37] [Is This Your Agent? Identity Watermarking for Agent Systems in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24382)
*Yuwei Zhong,Yu Cheng,Jiuan Zhou,Weizhi Liu,Yi Xie,Yongkang Hu,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 动态环境下agent身份水印与鲁棒恢复，属agent可靠性与安全控制方向。

TL;DR: 提出面向动态智能体系统的身份水印框架DASMark，通过动作保持嵌入与偏移鲁棒恢复，在动态和攻击场景下实现高精度水印解码并保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中智能体持续更新记忆、技能和运行时配置，轨迹不断演化，现有文本与智能体水印难以稳定嵌入，且嵌入水印易因环境变化难以恢复，因此需要面向动态智能体系统的持久身份认证水印方法。

Method: DASMark包含动作保持嵌入（APE）和偏移鲁棒恢复（SRR）；APE从多样交互轨迹中学习语义相似但可区分的决策表达通道以实现稳定嵌入，SRR缓解上下文引发的偏移并提升动态环境下水印恢复稳定性。

Result: 动态设置下比特解码准确率达98.5%，攻击场景下仍保持95.2%准确率，且任务效用与未加水印智能体相当。

Conclusion: DASMark能够在动态智能体系统中实现稳定嵌入与鲁棒恢复，兼顾身份认证性能与任务效用。

中文摘要：部署在动态环境中的智能体系统会持续更新其记忆、技能和运行时配置，从而产生不断演化的轨迹，并给持续身份认证带来挑战。现有的文本水印和智能体水印方法难以在动态环境下实现稳定的水印嵌入，同时嵌入的水印可能因环境变化而变得难以恢复。本文提出DASMark，一种面向动态智能体系统的身份水印框架。DASMark引入动作保持嵌入（APE），它从多样化的交互轨迹中学习语义相似但可区分的决策表达通道，以在动态系统中实现稳定的水印嵌入。它进一步引入偏移鲁棒恢复（SRR），以缓解上下文引发的偏移，并提高动态环境下水印恢复的稳定性。在动态设置中，DASMark达到98.5%的比特解码准确率，并在攻击场景下保持95.2%的准确率，同时保持与未加水印智能体相当的任务效用。

Original abstract: Agent systems deployed in dynamic environments continuously update their memories, skills, and runtime configurations, leading to evolving trajectories and challenges for persistent identity authentication. Existing text and agent watermarking methods struggle to achieve stable watermark embedding under dynamic environments, while embedded watermarks may become difficult to recover due to environmental changes. This paper proposes DASMark, an identity watermarking framework for dynamic agent systems. DASMark introduces Action-Preserving Embedding (APE), which learns semantically similar but distinguishable decision-expression channels from diverse interaction trajectories to enable stable watermark embedding in dynamic systems. It further introduces Shift-Robust Recovery (SRR) to mitigate context-induced shifts and improve watermark recovery stability under dynamic environments. In dynamic settings, DASMark achieves 98.5% bit-decoding accuracy and maintains 95.2% accuracy under attack scenarios, while preserving task utility comparable to unwatermarked agents.

</details>


### [38] [IF-MAP: Influence-Function-Inspired Agent Prioritization in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24383)
*Jie Mao,Pingchen Lu,Zikun Qu,Jiahang Sun,Xiangyi Wang,Zhiwei Shang,Zhiyong Wang,Guojun Xiong,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 影响函数启发的多agent系统agent优先级选择，含代码生成工作流，可用于agent改进资源的分配。

TL;DR: IF-MAP 通过受控提示扰动和局部响应建模，以黑盒方式为多智能体系统中的智能体排序，从而用更少 token 获得更大的系统级改进收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进多智能体系统中的所有智能体成本过高，而失败归因不能直接反映改进某个智能体可带来的系统级收益，因此需要识别最值得优先改进的智能体。

Method: IF-MAP 对每个智能体施加受控语义提示扰动，测量端到端损失变化，在低维提示嵌入子空间中拟合正则化局部响应模型，并以估计的响应范数作为优先级分数；该方法无需模型参数或梯度。

Result: 在六个多智能体工作流中，优化所选智能体提示的宏平均提升为 3.81 个百分点，高于均匀选择的 1.85 个百分点；在五个目标模型上，升级所选智能体带来 7.62–16.42 个百分点提升，高于均匀选择的 3.81–8.62 个百分点；相较优化或升级所有智能体，IF-MAP 分别减少 Qwen3-8B 与 DeepSeek-V3.2 的 token 使用量 74.2% 和 35.2%，并减少目标模型 token 使用量 63.6%–64.9%。

Conclusion: IF-MAP 能有效识别有前景的智能体并指导改进投入，从而以更低成本获得更大的端到端性能收益。

中文摘要：在基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，改进每一个智能体可能成本高得难以承受，因此重要的是识别应优先改进哪个智能体，以便在资源有限时获得最大的系统级收益。失败归因可以为这一决策提供信息，但智能体对失败的责任并不直接揭示改进它所能实现的系统级收益。因此，我们提出 IF-MAP，一种受影响函数启发的方法，用于识别具有较高系统级改进潜力的智能体。对于每个智能体，IF-MAP 在受控语义提示扰动下测量端到端损失变化，在低维提示嵌入子空间中拟合正则化局部响应模型，并使用估计的响应范数作为优先级分数。这一黑盒流程无需访问模型参数或梯度，并产生可指导后续改进工作的智能体优先级。我们在涵盖代码生成、问答和数学推理的六个多智能体工作流上评估 IF-MAP，并将相同的优先级用于提示优化和模型升级。优化所选智能体的提示实现了 3.81 个百分点的宏平均提升，而均匀选择智能体为 1.85 个百分点。在五个目标模型上，升级所选智能体实现了 7.62–16.42 个百分点的宏平均提升，而均匀选择智能体为 3.81–8.62 个百分点。尽管存在探测开销，IF-MAP 相对于优化每个智能体，将 Qwen3-8B 和 DeepSeek-V3.2 的 token 使用量分别减少 74.2% 和 35.2%；相对于升级每个智能体，将目标模型 token 使用量减少 63.6%–64.9%。这些结果支持使用 IF-MAP 来识别有前景的智能体，并将改进工作导向更大的端到端性能收益。

Original abstract: Improving every agent in an LLM-based multi-agent system can be prohibitively costly, making it important to identify which agent should be prioritized to achieve the greatest system-level gain with limited resources. Failure attribution can inform this decision, but an agent's responsibility for failures does not directly reveal the system-level gains achievable by improving it. We therefore introduce IF-MAP, an influence-function-inspired method for identifying agents with high potential for system-level improvement. For each agent, IF-MAP measures end-to-end loss changes under controlled semantic prompt perturbations, fits a regularized local response model in a low-dimensional prompt-embedding subspace, and uses the estimated response norm as a priority score. This black-box procedure requires no access to model parameters or gradients and produces agent priorities that can guide subsequent improvement effort. We evaluate IF-MAP across six multi-agent workflows spanning code generation, question answering, and mathematical reasoning, using the same priorities for prompt optimization and model upgrades. Optimizing the selected agents' prompts achieves a macro-average gain of 3.81 percentage points, compared with 1.85 for uniform agent selection. Across five target models, upgrading the selected agents yields macro-average gains of 7.62-16.42 percentage points, compared with 3.81-8.62 for uniform agent selection. Despite its probing overhead, IF-MAP reduces Qwen3-8B and DeepSeek-V3.2 token use by 74.2% and 35.2%, respectively, relative to optimizing every agent, and reduces target-model tokens by 63.6-64.9% relative to upgrading every agent. These results support the use of IF-MAP to identify promising agents and direct improvement effort toward greater end-to-end performance gains.

</details>


### [39] [Bridging Exploration and Persona: Information-Directed Dueling for Test-Time Agent Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24385)
*Jie Mao,Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Hengquan Huang,Shuang Qiu,Min Zhang,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于对决bandit的测试时persona个性化，属agent在线自适应方法但较为边缘。

TL;DR: ID-TAP利用查询条件神经效用模型和Bradley-Terry探索器，在二元比较信号下进行大规模人设选择与测试时个性化，并在七个基准的十轮预算内取得最强总体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时个性化需要从少量交互中适应与查询相关的用户偏好；大规模离散人设空间下的在线选择及比较信号利用仍具挑战。

Method: 提出ID-TAP：以查询条件神经效用模型共享跨查询统计强度并保留查询特定分数，结合轻量级Bradley-Terry探索器评分挑战者，并通过相似度加权热启动迁移比较证据。

Result: 在七个用户条件生成基准及参考和基于模型的偏好模拟器下，固定十轮比较预算内，ID-TAP在所评估基线中总体性能最强。

Conclusion: 信息导向的挑战者选择是测试时人设适配的有效设计。

中文摘要：有效的测试时个性化要求LLM智能体能够从轻量级交互中适应依赖于查询的用户偏好。我们研究以候选回复之间的二元比较作为在线信号的人设选择问题。我们提出ID-TAP，一个受IDS启发、面向大型离散人设空间的可扩展神经决斗赌博机框架。ID-TAP将查询条件神经效用模型与轻量级Bradley-Terry探索器相结合，并通过相似度加权热启动在相关查询之间迁移比较证据。该神经模型在查询间共享统计强度，同时保留查询特定得分，并利用高效的BT更新对假设挑战者进行评分。在参考偏好模拟器和基于模型的偏好模拟器下的七个用户条件生成基准上，ID-TAP在固定的十轮比较预算内取得了所评估基线中最强的总体性能。这些结果支持将信息导向的挑战者选择作为测试时人设适配的有效设计。

Original abstract: Effective test-time personalization requires LLM agents to adapt to query-dependent user preferences from lightweight interaction. We study persona selection with binary comparisons between candidate responses as the online signal. We present ID-TAP, a scalable, IDS-inspired neural dueling-bandit framework for large discrete persona spaces. ID-TAP combines a query-conditioned neural utility model with a lightweight Bradley-Terry explorer, and transfers comparison evidence across related queries through similarity-weighted warm starts. The neural model shares statistical strength across queries while preserving query-specific scores, and efficient BT updates score hypothetical challengers. Across seven user-conditioned generation benchmarks under reference- and model-based preference simulators, ID-TAP achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the evaluated baselines within a fixed ten-round comparison budget. These results support information-directed challenger selection as an effective design for test-time persona adaptation.

</details>


### [40] [Hippo: Training-Free Agent Self-Evolution via Hippocampal Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24453)
*Yutong Song,Jiang Wu,Yuxuan Ren,Tony Qin,Baoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出无需训练参数的agent外部记忆自演化框架，在失败诊断下比较同任务多次尝试并合并/修订经验，SWE-bench Verified等实验对SemOpt式经验复用与验证机制高度可借鉴。

TL;DR: Hippo提出一种受海马选择性编码与重放启发的LLM智能体外部记忆框架，通过比较同一任务的多次尝试、合并重叠经验并用显式适用条件解决冲突，在不更新模型参数的情况下实现记忆学习与修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次尝试中成功可能来自错误推理，失败也可能掩盖正确策略，因此经验提取噪声大；同时，不进行整合的记忆累积会先提升后退化，甚至低于无记忆基线。

Method: Hippo比较同一任务的多次尝试以识别可复用决策并细化适用条件；在诊断出不足时学习，并将新经验与现有记忆比较，通过显式适用条件合并重叠、解决冲突，从而修订已有指导而无需为每条经验单独建条目。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、WebArena和Mind2Web上均取得性能提升，且保留的经验显著少于累积基线；增益覆盖所评估的不同能力基础模型。

Conclusion: 基于外部记忆的适应可在不更新模型参数的情况下有效改进LLM智能体，并且即使对高能力模型仍有价值。

中文摘要：受递归自我改进（RSI）启发，我们研究LLM智能体如何在不更新模型参数的情况下通过外部记忆进行学习。从单次尝试中提取有用经验很困难：有缺陷的推理可能产生成功，而糟糕的执行可能掩盖合理的策略。长期保持其价值也很困难：我们不进行整合的累积基线最初有所提升，但最终表现低于无记忆基线。我们的关键思想是比较同一任务的多次尝试，以识别可复用决策并细化其适用时机。我们提出Hippo，一个受选择性海马编码与重放启发的记忆框架，在诊断出不足时从这些比较中学习。它将所得经验与现有记忆进行比较，通过显式适用条件合并重叠并解决冲突。这允许修订先前的指导，而无需将每条经验存储为单独条目。在SWE-bench Verified、WebArena和Mind2Web上的实验显示，三种设置下均有性能提升，且保留的经验显著少于累积基线。这些增益扩展到所评估的不同能力基础模型，表明基于记忆的适应即使对高能力模型仍然有用。

Original abstract: Motivated by recursive self-improvement (RSI), we study how LLM agents can learn through external memory without updating model parameters. Extracting useful experience from a single attempt is difficult: flawed reasoning can produce success, while poor execution can obscure a sound strategy. Preserving its value over time is also difficult: our accumulation baseline without consolidation initially improves but eventually performs below a no-memory baseline. Our key idea is to compare attempts at the same task to identify reusable decisions and refine when they apply. We introduce Hippo, a memory framework inspired by selective hippocampal encoding and replay that learns from these comparisons when shortcomings are diagnosed. It compares the resulting experience with existing memory to merge overlaps and resolve conflicts through explicit applicability conditions. This allows prior guidance to be revised without storing every lesson as a separate entry. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified, WebArena, and Mind2Web show performance gains in all three settings and substantially fewer retained experiences than the accumulation baseline. The gains extend across the evaluated base models of varying capabilities, indicating that memory-based adaptation remains useful even for highly capable models.

</details>


### [41] [MELD: Recursive Self-Improvement via Hierarchical Coordination for Multi-Agent Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24454)
*Sizhe Tang,Zuyuan Zhang,Zeyu Fang,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent科学发现中的层级协调与测试时递归自我改进，Coordinator/Scientist双层策略联合训练可迁移到多agent分工与经验共演化设计。

TL;DR: MELD是一个层级式测试时训练框架，联合优化团队级Coordinator与个体Scientist策略，在22个MLGym和MLE-bench任务中的20个上取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体科学发现中，研究任务分配同时影响实验选择与智能体适应经验，团队组织与个体研究行为存在耦合学习问题。

Method: 提出MELD：Coordinator在共享推导树上生成搜索分支和研究指令，Scientist提出假设与执行计划并由共享冻结Executor执行；两级策略在共享平滑预算内最优目标下交替进行GRPO式更新，实现递归自我改进。

Result: 理论分析给出层级目标对齐、协调增益条件、私有适应一阶收益及有限预算遗憾界；在22个MLGym和MLE-bench任务中20个最佳，并在MLGym上取得最高聚合归一化分数，协调与研究使用4B骨干模型。

Conclusion: MELD能够在一个发现过程中联合适应团队级与个体级策略，使团队组织与个体研究行为共同演化，并在多任务上优于使用专有Scientist的多数基线。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型的多智能体科学发现引出一个耦合学习问题：研究任务分配既决定追求哪些实验，也决定智能体从哪些经验中适应。我们提出MELD，一个统一的层级式测试时训练框架，在单一发现过程中联合适应团队级Coordinator策略和智能体级个体Scientist策略。Coordinator在共享推导树上生成搜索分支与研究指令的联合组合，而Scientist提出假设和执行计划，并由共享的冻结Executor实施。两个层级在共享的平滑预算内最优目标下接受交替的GRPO式参数更新，使团队组织与个体研究行为在递归自我改进循环中共同演化。我们的分析建立了该层级结构的目标对齐性，刻画了相对于独立并行发现取得协调增益的条件以及私有适应的一阶收益，并推导了适应模型的有限预算遗憾界。在22个MLGym和MLE-bench任务上，MELD在其中20个任务上表现最佳，并在MLGym上取得最高的聚合归一化分数，其协调与研究均使用4B骨干模型。大多数基线使用专有Scientist，且所有系统共享一个冻结Executor。

Original abstract: Multi-agent scientific discovery with large language models raises a coupled learning problem: research assignments determine both which experiments are pursued and the experiences from which agents adapt. We introduce MELD, a unified hierarchical test-time training framework that jointly adapts a team-level Coordinator policy and agent-level individual Scientist policies within a single discovery process. The Coordinator generates a joint portfolio of search branches and research directives over a shared derivation tree, while Scientists develop hypotheses and execution plans implemented by a shared frozen Executor. Both levels receive alternating GRPO-style parameter updates under a shared smooth best-of-budget objective, allowing team organization and individual research behavior to co-evolve in a recursive self-improvement loop. Our analysis establishes objective alignment for the hierarchy, characterizes conditions for coordination gains over independent parallel discovery and the first-order benefit of private adaptation, and derives a finite-budget regret bound for adapted models. On 22 MLGym and MLE-bench tasks, MELD is best on 20 of them and achieves the highest aggregate normalized scores on MLGym, using a 4B backbone for coordination and research. Most baselines use proprietary Scientists, and all systems share one frozen Executor.

</details>


### [42] [Learning to Compose: Recursive Self-Improvement of Shared Executable Stacks for Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24464)
*Yangzhi Chen,Feiran Liu,Zishuo Wang,Zijian Jin,Xin Zhu,Lecheng Ruan,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 把共享可执行栈（工具、技能契约、交接、验证器）作为持久改进单元，用执行轨迹做失败归因并生成缺失工具/修复，配回归门控，与自动代码优化agent的工具库演进机制直接同构。

TL;DR: Recursia 将共享可执行栈作为递归自我改进单元，通过执行轨迹归因、组件生成/修复与多重门控提升组合任务成功率，并在仿真和真实 Franka 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人技能单独有效但在组合时容易失败，原因包括工具能力缺失、完成信号失真以及技能间状态前提冲突。

Method: Recursia 以共享可执行栈而非单个任务程序为持续改进单元，整合工具、带执行契约的技能、交接与验证器；利用源侧执行轨迹进行失败归因并生成缺失工具或修复共享组件，再经通用性检查、机制测试及配对选择和回归门控迭代，最终冻结栈并通过声明式任务适配器复用。

Result: 仿真中较 ASPIRE 在 Pos 扰动下提升 5.8 个百分点、在 Task 扰动下提升 15.1 个百分点；三个物理组合目标任务平均成功率较 Skill Document Only 基线提升 40 个百分点；分析区分了局部机制改进与完整任务成功，并刻画了反馈条件化编排成本。

Conclusion: 共享可执行接口可作为机器人编程中组合迁移的一种具体机制。

中文摘要：单独胜任的机器人技能在组合时可能失败：工具可能缺少所需能力，完成信号可能错误表示物理进展，且一个技能可能留下违反其后继技能前提条件的状态。我们提出 Recursia，一个递归自我改进框架，它将共享的可执行栈而非单个任务程序视为持续改进的单元。该栈集成了工具、带执行契约的技能、交接和验证器。源侧执行轨迹指导失败归因以及缺失工具的生成或对现有共享组件的修复。通用性检查、机制测试以及配对选择和回归门控决定哪些更改成为下一个父代。所得栈被冻结，并通过声明式任务适配器复用，使源自源侧的更改能够支持新的组合，而无需目标侧代码修复。实验涵盖 LIBERO-Pro Long 仿真和配备 Franka Hand 的物理 Franka FR3。在仿真中，Recursia 在 Pos 扰动下将成功率较 ASPIRE 提高 5.8 个百分点，在 Task 扰动下提高 15.1 个百分点。在三个物理组合目标任务上，它将平均成功率较 Skill Document Only 基线提高 40 个百分点。对组成执行、候选拒绝和推理驱动因素的分析区分了局部机制改进与完整任务成功，并刻画了反馈条件化编排的成本。这些结果考察了共享可执行接口作为机器人编程中组合迁移的一种具体机制。

Original abstract: Individually competent robot skills can fail when composed: tools may lack a required capability, completion signals may misrepresent physical progress, and one skill may leave a state that violates its successor's prerequisites. We introduce Recursia, a recursive self-improvement framework that treats the shared executable stack, rather than individual task programs, as the persistent unit of improvement. The stack integrates tools, skills with execution contracts, handoffs, and verifiers. Source-side execution traces guide failure attribution and the generation of missing tools or repairs to existing shared components. Generality checks, mechanism tests, and paired selection and regression gates determine which changes become the next parent. The resulting stack is frozen and reused through declarative task adapters, allowing source-derived changes to support new compositions without target-side code repair. Experiments cover LIBERO-Pro Long simulation and a physical Franka FR3 equipped with a Franka Hand. In simulation, Recursia improves success over ASPIRE by 5.8 percentage points under Pos perturbations and 15.1 points under Task perturbations. Across three physical compositional target tasks, it improves mean success over the Skill Document Only baseline by 40 percentage points. Analyses of constituent execution, candidate rejection, and inference drivers distinguish local mechanism improvements from complete task success and characterize the cost of feedback-conditioned orchestration. These results examine shared executable interfaces as a concrete mechanism for compositional transfer in robot programming.

</details>


### [43] [HABIT-Bench: Evaluating Habit Induction from Longitudinal Weak Evidence in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24520)
*Junming Zhang,Rubin Wei,Xu Cheng,Hao Sun,Jiarui Wang,Ruoxi Cheng,Xiquan Wang,Peisong Wu,Mingsong Chen,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程交互中从弱证据归纳用户习惯的agent记忆基准，其多会话经验归纳与例外/漂移评估思路可迁移到agent记忆与经验适用条件建模。

TL;DR: 提出 HABIT-Bench，评估 LLM 智能体记忆系统能否从弱证据推断用户习惯并用于后续请求；最佳记忆方法仅达 33.61% 宏平均准确率，显示显著能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期交互中用户习惯蕴含隐式信息，但单次观察证据不完整，仅保留历史交互不足以让智能体正确推断和应用习惯。

Method: 构建 HABIT-Bench：基于任务导向对话数据和受控习惯图生成跨食品、金融、软件、旅行领域的合成用户历史，使用四选一任务评估习惯推断与应用，涵盖临时例外、习惯变化和未接受建议，并进行人工审核；用 Qwen3-235B-A22B 和 GLM-5.3-Flash 评估八种记忆方法。

Result: 最佳记忆方法在四个领域取得 33.61% 宏平均准确率；即使直接提供标注证据，性能仍有限。

Conclusion: 结果表明，获取过去交互与推断并恰当使用用户习惯之间仍存在持续差距。

中文摘要：在与大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的长期交互中，用户通过其选择和行动显露习惯。这些习惯捕获了智能体必须跨对话推断的隐式用户信息。然而，单次观察可能仅提供关于某个习惯或其适用场景的部分证据。仅保留过去交互并不能确保智能体能够推断习惯并恰当应用。我们引入 HABIT-Bench，用于评估智能体记忆系统从弱证据中推断习惯并将其应用于后续请求的能力。我们从任务导向对话数据和受控习惯图构建合成用户历史。这些历史跨越食品、金融、软件和旅行领域数百个会话。四选一任务评估智能体推断用户习惯并恰当应用的能力。任务包括临时例外、习惯变化以及用户未接受的建议。抽样任务经过独立人工审核。我们使用 Qwen3-235B-A22B 和 GLM-5.3-Flash 评估八种记忆方法。表现最佳的记忆方法在四个领域上达到 33.61% 的宏平均准确率。即使直接提供标注证据，性能仍然有限。这些结果揭示了在获取过去交互与推断并恰当使用用户习惯能力之间持续存在的差距。

Original abstract: In long-term interactions with large language model (LLM) agents, users reveal habits through their choices and actions. These habits capture implicit user information that agents must infer across conversations. However, individual observations may provide only partial evidence about a habit or when it applies. Retaining past interactions alone does not ensure that agents can infer habits and apply them appropriately. We introduce HABIT-Bench to evaluate how well agent memory systems infer habits from weak evidence and apply them to later requests. We construct synthetic user histories from task-oriented dialogue data and controlled habit graphs. The histories span hundreds of sessions across food, finance, software, and travel. Four-choice tasks assess agents' ability to infer user habits and apply them appropriately. They include temporary exceptions, changes in habits, and suggestions that users have not accepted. Sampled tasks undergo independent human review. We evaluate eight memory methods with Qwen3-235B-A22B and GLM-5.3-Flash. The best-performing memory method achieves 33.61\% macro accuracy across the four domains. Performance remains limited even when annotated evidence is supplied directly. These results expose a persistent gap between access to past interactions and the ability to infer and appropriately use user habits.

</details>


### [44] [CADS: Measuring the Incremental Contribution of LLM Proposers in Sequential Biological Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24597)
*Guanxing Chen,Nihui Shao,Haohuai He,Zhenchao Tang,Pengwei Hu,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 32 pages, 5 figures; supplementary material included

Selection reason: 用e-process与FDR控制度量LLM提议者相对无LLM基座的净增益，其消除harness/采样伪影的评测方法论可迁移到自动优化agent的收益与speedup可靠验证。

TL;DR: CADS用错误控制框架比较LLM提议器与相同无LLM回路；DepMap模拟显示原始命中计数有偏，Qwen2.5-7B初始净增益未在扩大重复中持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估依赖原始命中计数，混淆提议器价值与底层算法执行框架的发现能力；自适应采样和受损实验读数使反馈比较缺乏错误发现保证。

Method: 提出CADS，以截断似然比界、e过程和完整族e-BH控制终端错误发现率，在任意依赖、自适应采样和可选停止下量化相对于相同无LLM回路的增量价值。

Result: DepMap模拟揭示仅无LLM框架即可通过反馈门控，原始来源计数高估LLM贡献；20种子下Qwen2.5-7B净贡献由+0.35（区间含零）升至+2.90（区间高于零），但50种子匹配配额重复未复现。

Conclusion: 应以分别认证集合之间、相对无LLM回路的净增益衡量提议器贡献。

中文摘要：大语言模型正越来越多地用于指导序贯生物筛选，然而标准评估依赖原始命中计数，这会把提议器的价值与底层算法执行框架的发现能力混为一谈。自适应采样和受损的实验读数也使标准反馈比较缺乏错误发现保证。我们提出认证式智能体发现筛选（Certified Agentic Discovery Screens，CADS），这是一种受错误控制的测量框架，用于量化提议器相对于相同的无大语言模型回路的增量价值。通过整合截断似然比界、e过程以及完整族e-BH，CADS在所述零假设和污染条件下控制终端错误发现率，尽管存在任意依赖性、自适应采样和可选停止。在基于DepMap的模拟中，CADS揭示了两个评估假象：仅无大语言模型执行框架本身就通过了反馈门控；原始来源计数高估了大语言模型贡献，因为大语言模型的选择取代了回退发现。在初始20个随机种子上，向Qwen2.5-7B提供已测试候选历史，将其净贡献从+0.35（95%区间覆盖零）转变为+2.90（区间高于零）。这一初始净效应在50个随机种子的匹配配额重复实验中未能持续。这些结果支持将分别认证集合之间测量的、相对于无大语言模型回路的净增益，作为提议器贡献的适当度量。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to guide sequential biological screens, yet standard evaluations rely on raw hit counts that conflate a proposer's value with the discovery capacity of the underlying algorithmic harness. Adaptive sampling and corrupted experimental readouts also leave standard feedback comparisons without a false-discovery guarantee. We present Certified Agentic Discovery Screens (CADS), an error-controlled measurement framework that quantifies a proposer's incremental value against an identical LLM-free loop. By integrating capped likelihood-ratio bounds, e-processes, and complete-family e-BH, CADS controls the terminal false discovery rate under the stated null and corruption conditions despite arbitrary dependence, adaptive sampling, and optional stopping. In DepMap-backed simulations, CADS exposes two evaluation artifacts: the LLM-free harness alone clears the feedback gate, and raw origin counts overstate LLM contributions because LLM picks displace fallback discoveries. On the initial 20 seeds, supplying tested-candidate history to Qwen2.5-7B shifts its net contribution from +0.35, with a 95% interval covering zero, to +2.90, with the interval above zero. This initial net effect did not persist in a 50-seed matched-quota replication. These results support net gain over an LLM-free loop, measured between separately certified sets, as the appropriate measure of proposer contribution.

</details>


### [45] [HSPR: Recovering Software-Agent Benchmark Scores via Hierarchical Posterior Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24611)
*Wanyi Chen,Wanqin Shen,Yuanchen Shi,Zhensu Sun,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: 面向SWE-bench族软件agent的分层后验分数恢复与自适应任务选取，可显著降低agent评测成本，对SemOpt的benchmark评测策略有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: HSPR用分层后验恢复在少量任务预算下保持软件智能体排名并降低评分误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整基准评估昂贵；小子集虽可保持排名但分数不准，且相同源响应的任务对新目标智能体结果可能不同。

Method: 提出HSPR，结合源拟合响应因子与不确定组成功率；用目标观测更新后验，以后验引导采集，准则为全分数后验方差期望一步缩减，最终取全分数后验中位数并源选择校准。

Result: 在SWE-bench家族四个任务库10%预算下，HSPR在三个库MAE最低、四个库MaxErr最低；移除组不确定性或替换自适应查询使宏观MAE升高；5-20%预算内宏观MAE下降。

Conclusion: HSPR能在小任务预算下实现更准确的智能体评分并保持比较有效性。

中文摘要：在完整基准上评估软件智能体成本高昂。一个小的任务子集可以在产生不准确分数的同时保持智能体排名。历史响应提供了有用的结构，但具有相同源响应的任务对于新的目标智能体可能产生不同结果。我们引入分层分数后验恢复（HSPR），它将源拟合的响应因子与不确定的组成功率相结合。观测到的目标结果更新目标潜在状态和组成功率上的后验；后验引导的采集选择后续任务。其基于分数的准则等于全分数后验方差的一步期望缩减。已观测成功和未查询任务的后验预测计数产生全分数后验。最终估计使用其中位数，并采用源选择的校准。在SWE-bench家族的四个任务库上，以10%任务预算，HSPR在所比较方法中于三个库上取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE），并在所有四个库上取得最低最大绝对误差（MaxErr）。移除组不确定性或替换自适应查询会在各自对照中增加宏观MAE。宏观MAE在5-20%预算范围内下降。

Original abstract: Evaluating software agents on a full benchmark is expensive. A small task subset can preserve agent rankings while yielding inaccurate scores. Historical responses provide useful structure, but tasks with identical source responses can have different outcomes for a new target agent. We introduce Hierarchical Score Posterior Recovery (HSPR), which combines source-fitted response factors with uncertain group success rates. Observed target outcomes update the posteriors over the target latent state and group rates; posterior-guided acquisition selects subsequent tasks. Its score-based criterion equals the expected one-step reduction in full-score posterior variance. Observed successes and posterior-predictive counts for unqueried tasks yield a full-score posterior. The final estimate uses its median with source-selected calibration. At a 10% task budget on four task banks from the SWE-bench family, HSPR achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on three banks and the lowest maximum absolute error (MaxErr) on all four among the methods compared. Removing group uncertainty or replacing adaptive queries increases macro MAE in the respective controls. Macro MAE decreases across 5-20% budgets.

</details>


### [46] [Selm: Self-Evolving Library Management for Multi-Skill Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24630)
*Mengxi Hu,Yuxuan Hu,Jinbo Su,Ke Wang,Honghao Shi,Wenli Wu,Cuiping Li,Hong Chen,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将技能演化形式化为库级管理，联合检索、Map-Reduce式更新与生命周期剪枝，与自动代码优化agent的经验/优化策略库积累复用直接相关。

TL;DR: SELM将技能演化建模为技能库级管理，联合技能检索、更新与生命周期控制，以提升跨领域技能复用、积累和智能体任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能演化方法缺乏对演化技能的系统化管理，导致智能体难以跨领域复用和积累技能，进而限制后续任务性能。

Method: SELM维护技能置信度和效用信号，联合管理技能检索、更新和生命周期；采用效用感知检索选择有用技能，使用Map-Reduce框架从任务特定经验生成针对性更新，并通过生命周期控制根据有效性保留或移除技能。

Result: 在四个涵盖多样任务和执行环境的智能体基准上评估，SELM在大多数设置中持续优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 将技能演化作为库级管理，并联合检索、更新与生命周期控制，可使技能库持续积累有用经验并跨任务保留和修剪技能，从而提升智能体在多样领域复杂任务上的表现。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地将技能作为获取任务特定知识的一种轻量级方式。近期工作使技能能够从任务轨迹中演化。然而，这些方法通常缺乏对演化技能的系统化管理，使智能体难以跨领域复用和积累技能，从而限制了后续任务上的表现。为了跨任务可靠地积累、复用和维护有用技能，我们提出SELM（Self-Evolving Library Management），一个将技能演化表述为库级管理的框架。SELM通过维护技能置信度和效用信号，联合管理技能检索、更新和生命周期。它引入效用感知检索来选择有用技能，引入Map-Reduce框架从任务特定经验生成有针对性的更新，并引入生命周期控制以根据技能有效性保留或移除技能。这些机制使技能库能够持续积累有用经验，同时在跨任务中保留和修剪技能，提升智能体在多样领域复杂任务上的表现。我们在四个涵盖多样任务和执行环境的智能体基准上评估SELM，SELM在大多数设置中持续优于对比方法。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills as a lightweight way to acquire task-specific knowledge. Recent work enables skills to evolve from task trajectories. However, these methods often lack systematic management of evolved skills, making it difficult for agents to reuse and accumulate skills across domains, thus limiting performance on subsequent tasks.To reliably accumulate, reuse, and maintain useful skills across tasks, we present SELM (Self-Evolving Library Management), a framework that formulates skill evolution as library-level management. SELM jointly manages skill retrieval, updating, and lifecycle by maintaining skill confidence and utility signals. It introduces utility-aware retrieval to select useful skills, a Map-Reduce framework to generate targeted updates from task-specific experience, and lifecycle controls to retain or remove skills based on their effectiveness. These mechanisms enable the skill library to continuously accumulate useful experience while preserving and pruning skills across tasks, improving agent performance on complex tasks across diverse domains. We evaluate SELM on four agent benchmarks covering diverse tasks and execution environments, and SELM consistently outperforms in most settings.

</details>


### [47] [Good Judgment, Bad Advice: Decision-Model Controllers Raise Agent Cost Without Improving Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24649)
*Naman Vats*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/namanvats/decision-model-controllers

Selection reason: 在终端agent中受控比较决策控制器（规则/小模型/安慰剂）对成本与成功率的影响，给出“信号有信息但干预无效”的负面实证，对agent harness中的停止/验证决策设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上固定智能体循环、仅替换控制器，Jev 1.13 未提升通过率，反而使每次尝试成本增加 19%；其离线排序较好但校准差，决策信号未转化为有效干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅在确定性决策点调用的类型化决策模型 Jev 1.13，能否在不降低 Claude Sonnet 5.5 终端智能体表现的前提下降低其成本。

Method: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上进行预先指定研究，固定同一智能体循环，仅改变控制器：无控制器、确定性规则、小型 LLM Claude Haiku 5.5、Jev，以及按 Jev 开发阶段选择频率采样的安慰剂；在 21 个留出任务、315 次试验中比较成本与通过率，并进行离线评估。

Result: Jev 使每次尝试成本提高 19%（几何均值比 1.19，95% CI 1.02–1.36），通过率相同（73.0% vs. 73.0%）；比小型 LLM 控制器便宜（比值 0.81），但比安慰剂贵 22%，未检测到通过率差异。离线 AUROC 为 0.78，优于规则的 0.62 和小型 LLM 的 0.60，但校准差：实际 87% 运行通过时，其平均完成概率仅 0.50，导致要求验证已正确的工作；无控制器选择放弃运行。

Conclusion: 有信息量的决策信号没有成为有用的干预；需要进一步讨论何种条件才能使此类信号转化为有效控制。

中文摘要：Agent 脚手架越来越多地将小决策（是否停止、验证或改变方法）委托给更便宜的模型。我们追问：一个仅在确定性决策点被咨询的类型化决策模型 Jev 1.13，是否能让 Claude Sonnet 5.5 终端智能体变得更便宜而不变得更差。在一项于 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上预先指定的研究中，我们固定一个智能体循环，仅改变控制器：无控制器、确定性规则、回答相同问题的小型 LLM（Claude Haiku 5.5）、Jev，以及一个安慰剂控制器——它在相同时刻按 Jev 开发阶段的选择频率进行采样。在 21 个留出任务（315 次试验）上，Jev 将每次尝试的成本提高了 19%（几何均值比 1.19，95% CI 1.02–1.36），而通过率相同（73.0% 对 73.0%）。Jev 比小型 LLM 控制器更便宜（比值 0.81），但比安慰剂贵 22%，且各臂之间未检测到通过率差异。离线评估中，Jev 对完成请求的排序优于替代方案（AUROC 0.78，规则为 0.62，小型 LLM 为 0.60），但校准很差：当 87% 的这些运行通过时，其判断运行已完成的平均概率仅为 0.50，因此它要求智能体验证已经正确的工作。没有任何控制器选择放弃运行。有信息量的决策信号并未转化为有用的干预；我们讨论了这需要什么。

Original abstract: Agent scaffolds increasingly delegate small decisions (whether to stop, verify, or change approach) to cheaper models. We ask whether a typed decision model, Jev 1.13, consulted only at deterministic decision points, makes a Claude Sonnet 5.5 terminal agent cheaper without making it worse. In a pre-specified study on Terminal-Bench 2.1, we hold one agent loop fixed and vary only the controller: none, deterministic rules, a small LLM (Claude Haiku 5.5) answering the same questions, Jev, and a placebo that samples Jev's development-phase choice frequencies at the same moments. On 21 held-out tasks (315 trials), Jev raised cost per attempt by 19% (geometric-mean ratio 1.19, 95% CI 1.02-1.36) at an identical pass rate (73.0% vs. 73.0%). Jev was cheaper than the small-LLM controller (ratio 0.81) but 22% more expensive than the placebo, and no pass-rate difference between arms was detectable. Offline, Jev ranked finish requests better than the alternatives (AUROC 0.78 vs. 0.62 for rules and 0.60 for the small LLM) but was poorly calibrated: its mean probability that a run was complete was 0.50 when 87% of those runs passed, so it asked the agent to verify work that was already correct. No controller ever chose to abandon a run. Informative decision signals did not become useful interventions; we discuss what that would require.

</details>


### [48] [Neuro-Inspired Inhibitory Message Passing for Accurate and Token-Efficient Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24718)
*Nima H. Siboni,Magdalena Springer,Vahid Rostami*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出免训练的多 agent 抑制式消息传递路由，显著提升准确率并降低 token，对多 agent 协作与上下文分配有可迁移启发。

TL;DR: 提出免训练的抑制性消息传递路由，按证据句和接收者去除冗余信息，从而提升多智能体推理准确率并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作需要通信来分布检索和工具获得的信息，但消息累积会使重复信息挤占稀少且关键的决定性证据，因此需要让关键证据在每个智能体上下文中保持可见。

Method: 受皮层回路中抑制选择性压制共享输入而保留弱信号敏感性的原则启发，设计抑制性消息传递：一种免训练通信路由器，对每个证据句子和每个接收者分别取消冗余证据；每轮仅转发消息中重述的证据句，至多一次送达自身证据缺少该句的接收者，并排除发送者的推理和答案。

Result: 相较标准消息传递，在三个基准上同伴组准确率提升39-77个百分点，每次运行token数减少10-46%；收益在七个40亿至320亿参数开源模型、两个商业模型、最多64个智能体以及点对点、编排器-工作者和多级分层等多智能体架构中均保持。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持一种可扩展设计模式，可实现更准确且token更高效的多智能体推理。

中文摘要：多智能体系统有望通过汇集超出任何单个智能体所能获取的信息来解决问题。检索和工具使用可以将这些信息分布到不同智能体上，使通信成为协作的关键。一个核心挑战是：随着消息不断累积，如何在每个智能体的上下文中保持关键证据可见。重复信息可能挤占稀少但具有决定性的证据。皮层回路模型提出了应对这种失衡的原则：抑制可以选择性地压制共享输入，同时保留对较弱信号的敏感性。我们将这一原则转化为抑制性消息传递，这是一种免训练的通信路由器，针对每个证据句子和每个接收者分别取消冗余证据。每一轮中，它转发智能体消息中重述的证据句子，将每个证据句子至多一次传递给自身证据中缺少该句子的接收者，并排除发送者的推理和答案。与标准消息传递相比，在三个基准上，同伴组中的准确率提升了39-77个百分点，每次运行的token数下降了10-46%。这些收益在七个参数量从40亿到320亿的开源模型和两个商业模型上均成立，在最多64个智能体的群体中成立，并且在包括点对点、编排器-工作者和多级分层组织在内的多智能体架构中成立。这些发现支持一种可扩展的设计模式，用于实现更准确且token更高效的多智能体推理。

Original abstract: Multi-agent systems promise to solve problems by bringing together information beyond the reach of any individual agent. Retrieval and tool use can distribute this information across agents, making communication essential to collaboration. A central challenge is keeping crucial evidence visible in each agent's context as messages accumulate. Repeated information can crowd out rare, decisive evidence. Models of cortical circuits suggest a principle for addressing this imbalance: inhibition can selectively suppress shared inputs while preserving sensitivity to weaker signals. We adapt this principle into inhibitory message passing, a training-free communication router that cancels redundant evidence separately for each evidence sentence and receiver. Each round, it forwards evidence sentences restated in agents' messages, delivering each at most once to receivers whose own evidence lacks it and excluding the senders' reasoning and answers. Against standard message passing, accuracy in peer groups rose by 39-77 percentage points across three benchmarks, and tokens per run fell by 10-46%. Gains held across seven open models spanning four to thirty-two billion parameters and two commercial models, in groups up to sixty-four agents, and across multi-agent architectures including peer-to-peer, orchestrator-worker, and multilevel hierarchical organizations. These findings support a scalable design pattern for more accurate and token-efficient multi-agent reasoning.

</details>


### [49] [Where Natural Language Ends in Agents: Executable Skill Composition with MetaSkill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24723)
*Meng Shu,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Runke Liu,Kai Han,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures. Outstanding Paper Award at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Linguistic Principles for Foundation Models (LP4FM)

Selection reason: MetaSkill 用 DAG 运行时把 Skill 组合的依赖关系显式可执行化，直接对应 agent harness/workflow 与可审计编排设计。

TL;DR: MetaSkill 通过在 Skill 边界选择性形式化协调关系，以 DAG 运行时执行调用依赖与前驱文本绑定，在保留局部语言灵活性的同时实现可审计的多 Skill 组合，并在多任务与多模型比较中更受偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式 Skill 以自然语言承载任务知识，但多 Skill 组合缺乏持久执行顺序与上游输出交接机制，需要在协调层形式化而不侵蚀 Skill 内部判断。

Method: 提出边界选择性形式化与 MetaSkill 混合表示：保留完整 Skill 包和局部指令，将稳定引用、机器可检查约束、调用依赖及前驱文本绑定编译为 DAG 运行时；在 20 个多 Skill 制品任务、两个 LLM 上，与自然语言流程、提示可见图、AgentSkillOS 及配置工具智能体比较，并采用盲审与执行轨迹审计。

Result: 盲审偏好点估计在所有报告比较中有利；95% 任务自助法区间在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 下对 Prompt Graph、在 GLM-5.2 下对 OpenClaw 排除持平；GLM-5.2 审计轨迹中计划节点全部完成且声明先后约束全部满足。

Conclusion: MetaSkill 是组合开放式 Skill 的实用接口，兼顾局部语言灵活性、可执行依赖、显式前驱文本绑定与可审计协调。

中文摘要：Agent Skills 以自然语言表达开放式任务知识，但组合它们需要持久的执行顺序和上游输出交接。我们提出在 Skill 边界进行选择性形式化：具有稳定引用和机器可检查约束的协调关系变为可执行，而实质性任务判断仍保留在 Skill 内部。我们引入 MetaSkill，一种混合表示，在保留完整 Skill 包和局部指令的同时，通过有向无环图（DAG）运行时使调用依赖和前驱文本绑定可执行。该接口支持显式协调，而不规定每个 Skill 如何执行其局部任务。在 20 个多 Skill 制品任务和两个 LLM 上，我们比较自然语言流程、提示可见图和由每个任务共同的冻结计划派生出的 MetaSkill，并使用相同的指定 Skill 包。额外比较评估 AgentSkillOS（使用与 MetaSkill 相同的可用 Skill 包），以及使用其配置工具的智能体系统。盲审制品判断在所有报告的对照中给出有利的偏好点估计，其中 95% 任务自助法区间在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 下对 Prompt Graph、在 GLM-5.2 下对 OpenClaw 排除持平。在经审计的 GLM-5.2 执行轨迹中，所有计划节点均完成，所有声明的先后约束均满足。总之，这些发现支持 MetaSkill 作为组合开放式 Skill 的实用接口，将局部语言灵活性与可执行依赖、显式前驱文本绑定和可审计协调相结合。代码见：https://github.com/TokenRhythm/opensquilla/tree/paper/metaskill-lp4fm-2026

Original abstract: Agent Skills express open-ended task knowledge in natural language, but composing them requires persistent execution order and upstream-output handoffs. We propose selective formalization at Skill boundaries: coordination relations with stable references and machine-checkable constraints become executable, while substantive task judgments remain within Skills. We introduce MetaSkill, a hybrid representation that preserves complete Skill packages and local instructions while making invocation dependencies and predecessor-text bindings executable through a directed acyclic graph (DAG) runtime. This interface supports explicit coordination without prescribing how each Skill performs its local task. Across 20 multi-Skill artifact tasks and two LLMs, we compare natural-language procedures, prompt-visible graphs, and MetaSkill derived from a common frozen plan per task, using the same designated Skill packages. Additional comparisons evaluate AgentSkillOS with the same available Skill packages as MetaSkill, as well as agent systems using their configured tools. Blinded artifact judgments yield favorable preference point estimates across all reported comparisons, with 95% task-bootstrap intervals excluding parity against Prompt Graph under DeepSeek-V4-Flash and OpenClaw under GLM-5.2. In audited GLM-5.2 execution traces, all planned nodes complete and all declared precedence constraints are satisfied. Together, these findings support MetaSkill as a practical interface for composing open-ended Skills, combining local linguistic flexibility with executable dependencies, explicit predecessor-text bindings, and auditable coordination. Code is available at: https://github.com/TokenRhythm/opensquilla/tree/paper/metaskill-lp4fm-2026

</details>


### [50] [Can LLM Agents Resolve Service Failures? Simulating Multi-Turn Complaint Handling under Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24733)
*Wailing Ng,Mengze Hong,Chen Jason Zhang,Shiqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向客服投诉的多轮 agent benchmark 与信息不对称模拟，含能力诊断与 SFT/DPO 改进，方法论可迁移但应用偏垂直。

TL;DR: 论文提出首个投诉处理基准ServiceBench及APCS模拟与公正评估框架，发现LLM智能体在主动信息收集、对话引导和避免过早赔偿方面存在明显不足，而SFT与DPO后训练可显著改善小模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投诉处理情感要求高且仍依赖人工客服，现有LLM智能体在规则引导和信息获取方面虽有应用，但缺乏专门评测其投诉处理能力的基准与方法。

Method: 构建ServiceBench，包含2,500个场景、184个投诉类别和五个服务行业；提出非对称渐进式投诉模拟（APCS）以还原真实投诉处理过程，并采用基于公正的评估框架；进一步使用SFT和DPO进行针对性后训练。

Result: 实验显示LLM智能体难以有效处理投诉，缺乏主动收集信息和策略性引导对话至解决的能力，常过早提供赔偿；SFT与DPO后训练显著提升相关能力，使较小模型在若干结果上匹配或超越更大模型。

Conclusion: ServiceBench与APCS为投诉处理提供了专门评测和模拟手段，揭示了当前LLM智能体的关键短板，并证明针对性后训练是提升其投诉处理能力的有效路径。

中文摘要：LLM智能体被广泛用于服务聊天机器人中，以提供规则引导支持和信息获取，但情感要求高的投诉处理仍严重依赖人工客服。我们提出ServiceBench，据我们所知，这是首个专门面向投诉处理的基准，包含2,500个场景，覆盖184个投诉类别和五个服务行业。我们提出非对称渐进式投诉模拟（APCS）来模拟真实的投诉处理，并配套一个基于公正的评估框架。我们的实验表明，即使能力较强的LLM智能体也难以有效处理投诉。它们缺乏主动收集信息并策略性地引导对话走向解决的能力，常常过早诉诸赔偿。我们进一步表明，使用SFT和DPO进行有针对性的后训练可以显著提升这些能力，使较小模型在若干结果上匹配或超越大得多的模型。

Original abstract: LLM agents are widely adopted in service chatbots for rule-guided support and information access, yet emotionally demanding complaint handling still relies heavily on human agents. We introduce ServiceBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark dedicated to complaint handling, with 2,500 scenarios spanning 184 complaint categories and five service industries. We propose Asymmetric Progressive Complaint Simulation (APCS) to simulate realistic complaint handling, together with a justice-based evaluation framework. Our experiments reveal that even capable LLM agents struggle to handle complaints effectively. They lack the ability to proactively gather information and strategically guide conversations toward resolution, often resorting to premature compensation. We further show that targeted post-training with SFT and DPO substantially improves these capabilities, enabling a smaller model to match or surpass much larger models on several outcomes.

</details>


### [51] [Toward Collective Model Intelligence: Characterizing and Coordinating Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24739)
*SinapisAI Team,Jian-Dong Liu,Qun Dai,Zhendong Du,Guannan Lai,Hao-Yu Liu,Yi-Fan Ma,Zi-Yu Mao,Tengzhong Sun,Xin-Yi Zhang,Zi-Chen Zhao,Gelin Bian,Zongyuan Cai,Ju Cang,Zhangqin Chen,Shilei Gu,Guoding Ji,Taojian Jiang,Hao-Xuan Ma,Fan Wu,Lin-Xing Wu,Tao Yu,Xiao Zhang,Tianyi Zhou,Rong Gu,Ming Li,Zhi-Hao Tan,Han-Jia Ye,Yang Yu,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 35 pages, 14 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: 模型能力画像、按任务成本感知的模型选择与协作编排，对 agent 路由与多模型协同 harness 有参考价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出由 Model Eval、Mio 和 Mos 组成的系统方法，以能力评估、互补模型选择协作和高效服务调度实现集体模型智能，并在多个基准上以更低成本取得更高平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立 AI 模型能力多样互补，但模型数量增长不等于集体智能；需解决有限评估下的能力理解、互补模型组织协作以及资源约束下的高效调用问题。

Method: 提出三个组件：Model Eval 结合查询级性能估计与跨域预测构建能力画像；Mio 组织候选模型并支持任务条件化、成本感知的选择与协作；Mos 分离选择与执行，提供端点调度及共享模型部署分发。

Result: 有限评估下能力估计误差更低；在九个公开基准上，Mio 1.0 Lite 平均分最高、平均成本最低，每请求成本较最佳得分替代方法低 73.7-97.5%；预热共享缓存使模型文件检索最高加速 23.3 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作通过刻画和协调不断扩展模型宇宙中的多样能力，为 Collective Model Intelligence 奠定基础。

中文摘要：独立开发的 AI 模型会积累多样且互补的能力，但不断增长的模型集合并不会自动实现集体 AI。要弥合这一差距，需要在有限评估条件下理解模型能力、组织互补模型以协作完成多样任务，并在资源约束下高效执行模型调用。我们提出一种通过三个相互关联组件来追求集体模型智能的系统性方法。首先，Model Eval 通过将查询级性能估计与跨域预测相结合来构建基于证据的能力画像，从而减少对穷尽式评估的需求。其次，Mio（Models in One）组织互补的候选模型，并支持随着任务需求演进而进行以任务为条件、成本感知的选择与协作。第三，Mos（Model Serving OS）将模型选择与执行分离，并提供端点调度以及共享模型部署与分发。实验表明，在有限评估下能力估计误差更低。在九个公开基准上，Mio 1.0 Lite 在所比较方法中取得最高平均分和最低平均成本，其每请求成本比表现最佳的其他方法低 73.7-97.5%。预热的共享缓存将模型文件检索速度最高提升 23.3 倍。这项工作通过刻画并协调不断扩展的模型宇宙中的多样能力，为集体模型智能奠定了基础。

Original abstract: Independently developed AI models accumulate diverse and complementary capabilities, but a growing model collection does not automatically realize Collective AI. Addressing this gap requires understanding model capabilities with limited evaluation, organizing complementary models to collaborate on diverse tasks, and executing model calls efficiently under resource constraints. We present a systematic approach to pursuing Collective Model Intelligence through three connected components. First, Model Eval constructs evidence-based capability profiles by combining query-level performance estimation with cross-domain prediction, reducing the need for exhaustive evaluation. Second, Mio (Models in One) organizes complementary model candidates and supports task-conditioned, cost-aware selection and collaboration as task requirements evolve. Third, Mos (Model Serving OS) separates model selection from execution and provides endpoint scheduling and shared model deployment and distribution. Experiments show lower capability-estimation errors under limited evaluation. Across nine public benchmarks, Mio 1.0 Lite achieves the highest average score and lowest average cost among compared methods, with per-request costs 73.7-97.5% lower than those of the best-scoring alternatives. A prewarmed shared cache accelerates model-file retrieval by up to 23.3 times. This work lays a foundation for Collective Model Intelligence by characterizing and coordinating the diverse capabilities of an expanding model universe.

</details>


### [52] [ReTRACE: Feedback-Guided LLM Agent in a Chemistry Harness for Retrosynthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24744)
*Jinxiang Xie,Yijian Zhang,Qingyuan Yi,Jianyi Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 化学逆合成 LLM agent 置于工具编排 harness 中，含评估反馈学习、回溯与路线记忆，架构可迁移到代码优化 agent。

TL;DR: 提出 ReTRACE 智能体，将 LLM 与化学工具、单步预测引擎和多准则评估器结合，实现整条逆合成路线的生成、审计与迭代优化；在三个基准上高比例通过审计，并获得两项前瞻性湿实验的初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂逆合成需要对整条路线进行综合判断，涉及立体化学、官能团兼容性、成本和绿色性等多因素交互，现有单步预测、树搜索和路线评分仍不足以充分应对。

Method: 构建 ReTRACE，将 LLM 置于化学工作框架中，集成分子工具、互补的单步预测引擎、可购买起始原料库、路线记忆和多准则路线评估器；采用结合程序化检查与对抗性 LLM 审查的新指标评价路线，并与合成化学家判定比较；智能体在搜索中依据评估器反馈持续学习，诊断薄弱路线并回溯修正；使用 Claude Opus 4.8 在三个基准上测试，并开展两项前瞻性湿实验合成。

Result: 在三个基准上，ReTRACE 提出的前 10 条路线中至少有一条通过审计的目标比例分别为 87.4%、95.0% 和 86.7%；两项前瞻性湿实验合成表明，经专家修订后其路线建议可转化为可执行程序。

Conclusion: ReTRACE 展示了将 LLM 智能体与化学工具和多准则评估相结合用于复杂逆合成路线设计的潜力，并具备经专家修订后转化为实验可执行方案的可行性。

中文摘要：计算机辅助逆合成已通过单步预测、树搜索和路线评分取得进展，但复杂目标仍需要对整条路线进行判断，其中立体化学、官能团兼容性、成本和绿色性相互作用。在此，我们提出 ReTRACE（工具编排推理与上下文感知证据的逆合成），这是一种智能体，将大型语言模型（LLM）置于化学工作框架中，该框架整合分子工具、互补的单步预测引擎、可购买起始原料库、路线记忆和多准则路线评估器。路线由一种新指标评判，该指标结合程序化检查与对抗性 LLM 审查，并将其判定结果与合成化学家的判定进行比较。该智能体在搜索过程中从评估器反馈中持续学习，诊断薄弱路线并回溯以修正它们。使用 Claude Opus 4.8，在三个基准上，ReTRACE 提出的前 10 条路线中至少有一条通过审计的目标比例分别为 87.4%、95.0% 和 86.7%。两项前瞻性湿实验合成表明，经过专家修订后，ReTRACE 的路线建议可以转化为可执行程序。

Original abstract: Computer-aided retrosynthesis has advanced through single-step prediction, tree search and route scoring, but complex targets still require judgement over the whole route, where stereochemistry, functional-group compatibility, cost and greenness interact. Here we present \textbf{\mbox{ReTRACE}} (\textbf{RE}trosynthesis with \textbf{T}ool-orchestrated \textbf{R}easoning \textbf{A}nd \textbf{C}ontext-aware \textbf{E}vidence), an agent that places a large language model (LLM) in a chemistry harness that integrates molecular tools, complementary single-step prediction engines, a purchasable starting-material library, a route memory and a multi-criterion route evaluator. Routes are judged by a new metric that combines programmatic checks with adversarial LLM review, and its verdicts are compared with a synthetic chemist's. The agent learns continually from evaluator feedback during search, diagnosing weak routes and backtracking to revise them. With Claude Opus 4.8, at least one of the top 10 routes proposed by ReTRACE passed the audit for 87.4\%, 95.0\% and 86.7\% of targets on the three benchmarks. Two prospective wet-lab syntheses show that ReTRACE route proposals can be converted into executable procedures after expert revision.

</details>


### [53] [DialogMem: Evaluating Dialogue-Grounded Embodied Memory in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24767)
*SeongJun Jeong,Junghyun Kim,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 对话接地具身记忆 benchmark，系统分析记忆形成、检索与定位三处瓶颈，对 agent 长程记忆设计有一般性启发。

TL;DR: DialogMem 是一个基于时间对齐 RGB-D 观测与对话构建具身记忆的两阶段基准，覆盖 27 个真实 3D 场景、142 个回合、14,193 个 QA 对和 11,890 条导航指令；实验发现对话虽能显著提升 QA，却几乎不改善导航，暴露出对话—对象关联、正确条目检索和空间定位三个瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身记忆主要依赖视觉经验，尚未解决人类对话如何与智能体在环境中遇到的对象建立关联的问题，因此需要能整合观察与对话的具身记忆基准。

Method: 提出 DialogMem 两阶段基准：智能体从时间对齐的带位姿 RGB-D 观测流和对话流中形成记忆，并用于实例级物体导航与 QA；在统一的记忆形成、证据检索和推理框架下评估九种记忆表示，并进行受控诊断。

Result: 在 27 个真实 3D 场景的 142 个回合、14,193 个 QA 对和 11,890 条导航指令上，评估时追加对话显著提升 QA，但导航增益可忽略；导航随链接质量提高而改善，但完美链接下仍低于真值对象级记忆；检索正确对象条目和恢复其空间位置仍是瓶颈。

Conclusion: 对话关联具身记忆的关键瓶颈有三：形成阶段将对话关联并组织到对象级条目、检索正确条目、以及准确定位该条目。

中文摘要：以人为中心的具身智能体需要一种记忆，能够随时间整合其观察到的内容与人类告诉它的内容。然而，现有具身记忆主要建立在视觉经验之上，人类对话如何与这一经验中遇到的对象建立关联仍悬而未决。我们提出 DialogMem，一个两阶段基准：智能体从时间对齐的带位姿 RGB-D 观测流与对话流中构建记忆，随后利用该记忆进行实例级物体导航和问答（QA）。DialogMem 覆盖 27 个真实世界 3D 场景中的 142 个回合，包含 14,193 个 QA 对和 11,890 条导航指令。我们在一个统一框架内评估了九种记忆表示，该框架涵盖记忆形成、证据检索和面向任务输出的推理。实验表明，在评估时将对话追加到视觉记忆可显著提升 QA，但对导航带来的增益可以忽略，这表明相关对话存在于记忆中，却未与特定对象建立关联。受控诊断显示，导航性能随链接质量提高而改善，但即使在完美链接下仍低于真值对象级记忆。检索正确的对象条目并恢复其空间位置仍是进一步瓶颈。这些结果识别出对话关联具身记忆的三个瓶颈：在形成阶段将对话关联并组织到对象级条目、检索正确条目，以及准确定位该条目。

Original abstract: Embodied agents in human-centered environments require memory that integrates what they observe with what humans tell them over time. However, existing embodied memories are built primarily from visual experience, leaving open how dialogue with humans can be grounded in the objects encountered throughout that experience. We introduce DialogMem, a two-phase benchmark in which agents build memory from time-aligned streams of posed RGB-D observations and dialogue, and later use it for instance-level object navigation and question answering (QA). DialogMem provides 142 episodes across 27 real-world 3D scenes, with 14,193 QA pairs and 11,890 navigation instructions. We evaluate nine memory representations within a unified framework of memory formation, evidence retrieval, and reasoning for task outputs. In experiments, appending dialogue to visual memory at evaluation time substantially improves QA but yields negligible navigation gains, suggesting that relevant dialogue is present in memory but not grounded to specific objects. Controlled diagnostics show that navigation improves with link quality but falls short of ground-truth object-level memory even under perfect linking. Retrieving the correct object entry and recovering its spatial location remain further bottlenecks. These results identify three bottlenecks for dialogue-grounded embodied memory: grounding and organizing dialogue at object-level entries during formation, retrieving the correct entry, and localizing it accurately.

</details>


### [54] [IndustrialCAD-1400: Benchmarking Detailed Industrial Assemblies from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24789)
*Hebao Zhu,Kang An,Zhuoke Liao,Faqiang Qian,Ziliang Wang,Mingang Chen,Qibing Ren*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 文本到工业 CAD 装配 benchmark，以几何程序生成并由统一环境执行评估，与代码生成 agent 的可执行评测范式相通。

TL;DR: 提出 IndustrialCAD-1400，一个面向工业装配的文本到CAD基准，包含1,400项跨40个领域、400个设备类别和250个四案例设计族的请求，并通过Blender执行生成几何程序，以前视、等轴测和俯视图评估组件、布置、接口及数值规范满足情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业装配不仅要求形状可识别，还要求功能零件、接口、尺寸和服务特征共同满足详细请求；现有文本到CAD任务多集中于可识别零件几何，缺少对工业装配综合规范的评测。

Method: 构建 IndustrialCAD-1400：1,400项请求覆盖40个领域、400个设备类别和250个四案例设计族；族请求包含中位数17个具名组件或配置角色及6个显式数值目标，并设置受控变体测试尺寸变化与共享结构保留。任务参考制造商信息、业主规格和工程指南；模型生成几何程序，由统一Blender环境执行，产生场景、可选测量和标准化视图；采用前视、等轴测和俯视图进行请求评估，衡量可见组件、布置和接口满足情况，无需单一参考几何；五个分层面板保留完整设计族并恰好覆盖基准一次。

Result: 形成了可平衡比较的工业文本到CAD基准与评估协议，能够区分工业广度与受控数值响应性，并在不依赖单一参考几何的情况下评估组件、布置、接口和数值规范的联合满足情况；摘要未报告具体模型性能数值。

Conclusion: IndustrialCAD-1400将文本到CAD从可识别的零件几何扩展到在组件、接口、空间和数值联合规范下的工业装配合成。

中文摘要：工业装配所需的不只是一个可识别的形状：功能零件、接口、尺寸和服务特征必须共同满足一项详细请求。我们提出 IndustrialCAD-1400，一个面向该工业场景的文本到CAD基准。其1,400项请求将40个领域中的400个设备类别与250个四案例设计族相结合。族请求指定中位数为17个具名组件或配置角色以及六个显式数值目标；受控变体测试在保留共享结构的同时尺寸是否改变。任务构建参考了有记录的制造商信息、业主规格和工程指南。模型根据请求生成几何程序，并由统一的Blender环境执行这些程序以生成场景、可选测量和用于评估的标准化视图。对前视、等轴测和俯视图的请求评估衡量可见组件、布置和接口的满足情况，而不需要单一参考几何。五个分层面板保留完整设计族，同时恰好覆盖基准一次，从而支持平衡比较并将工业广度与受控数值响应性区分开来。总之，IndustrialCAD-1400将文本到CAD从可识别的零件几何扩展到在组件、接口、空间和数值联合规范下的工业装配合成。

Original abstract: Industrial assemblies require more than a recognizable shape: functional parts, interfaces, dimensions and service features must satisfy a detailed request together. We introduce IndustrialCAD-1400, a text-to-CAD benchmark for this industrial setting. Its 1,400 requests combine 400 equipment categories across 40 domains with 250 four-case design families. Family requests specify a median of 17 named component or configuration roles and six explicit numeric targets; controlled variants test whether dimensions change while shared structure is retained. Task construction draws on documented manufacturer information, owner specifications and engineering guidance. Models generate geometry programs from requests, and a common Blender environment executes them to produce scenes, optional measurements, and standardized views for evaluation. A request assessment of front, isometric, and top views measures visible component, arrangement, and interface fulfillment without requiring a single reference geometry. Five stratified panels preserve complete families while covering the benchmark exactly once, enabling balanced comparisons and separating industrial breadth from controlled numerical responsiveness. In conclusion, IndustrialCAD-1400 extends text-to-CAD from recognizable part geometry to industrial assembly synthesis under joint component, interface, spatial, and numerical specifications.

</details>


### [55] [Bellman Language Models: Solving the Recurring Decisions of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24871)
*Adarsh Vatsa,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 52 pages, 13 figures, 11 tables. Preregistrations: https://github.com/adarsh-vatsa/dtd-preregistration

Selection reason: 把 agent 决策视为 MDP 一次性求解并复用为控制器，在 τ²-bench 大幅减少模型调用，直接启发 agent harness 与效率优化。

TL;DR: 将有限智能体工作流中的决策离散化为状态并一次性求解马尔可夫决策过程，再作为模型外围控制器复用，可显著减少模型调用并提升速度、成本与可靠性，但对内容生成型任务帮助有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在有限工作流中会反复遇到相同状态，却每轮都从上下文或提示中重新推导决策，而不把决策视为优化问题，导致运行更慢、更贵且可靠性不足。

Method: 提出 Discretize, Then Decide：从工作流日志或智能体自身运行中把对话解析为离散状态，估计各结果概率与动作成本直至状态和概率收敛，求解马尔可夫决策过程；将解作为模型外围控制器，对状态中内容固定的动作直接执行，对未见状态回传 LLM；以这种方式运行的 LLM 称为贝尔曼语言模型（BLM）。

Result: 在 τ^2-bench telecom 上，无模型控制器解决 79% 任务；Qwen3.8-27B 作为 BLM 从 29% 升至 96%，模型调用减少 93%；Claude Opus 5.5 作为 BLM 将模型调用和每任务成本降低约 98%，并多解决 2 个任务；InventoryBench 上无模型策略优于所有已发表方法；在 SymPy 等以内容编写为难点而非动作选择的场景中，BLM 帮助不大。

Conclusion: 当智能体工作流的决策空间有界且可离散收敛时，将决策一次性优化并复用可显著提升效率、降低成本并增强可靠性；若瓶颈在于内容生成而非动作选择，则该方法的收益有限。

中文摘要：在智能体工作流中，尤其是有限的工作流中，一个普遍观察是：当我们将决策与生成分离时，LLM 的决策空间开始接近饱和——智能体会反复遇到相同的状态，而在足够多的对话之后，它不再遇到新的状态。LLM 通常不把这些决策当作优化问题，而是每一轮都从上下文/提示中重新推导决策。我们表明，在合理边界内将决策制定视为一个优化问题，一次性求解并复用已求解的决策，往往能使智能体更快、更便宜且更可靠。我们的方法称为 Discretize, Then Decide（离散化，然后决策），它从工作流自身日志等数据出发；当没有此类数据时，则从 LLM 智能体自身的运行出发。它把每个对话解析为离散状态，并估计每个结果的概率和每个动作的成本，直到不再出现新状态且概率不再变化。然后，它求解所得的马尔可夫决策过程。该解作为模型外围的控制器运行：发送内容在该状态中已固定的动作，例如工具调用或模板化回复；任何未见过的状态则回传给 LLM。我们将以这种方式运行的 LLM 称为贝尔曼语言模型（Bellman language model, BLM），名称来自理查德·贝尔曼（Richard Bellman）关于每个状态下最佳动作的方程。在 τ^2-bench telecom 上，无模型控制器解决了 79% 的任务。作为 BLM，开放模型 Qwen3.8-27B 从 29% 提升到 96%，模型调用减少 93%。对于自身可解决 96% 任务的 Claude Opus 5.5，将其作为 BLM 运行可将模型调用和每任务成本降低约 98%，并使其多解决 2 个任务。在 InventoryBench 上，无模型求解策略优于所有已发表方法。当困难之处在于编写内容而非选择动作时，例如解决 SymPy 问题，能够预先求解的决策很少，BLM 帮助不大。

Original abstract: A general observation in agentic workflows, especially bounded ones, is that the decision space of the LLM, when we separate a decision from generation, starts approaching saturation: the agent meets the same states again and again, and after enough conversations it stops meeting new ones. LLMs generally don't treat these decisions as an optimization problem and instead re-derive them from their context/prompt on every turn. We show that treating decision making within reasonable bounds as an optimization problem, solving it once, and reusing the solved decisions can often make an agent faster, cheaper and more reliable. Our approach, which we call Discretize, Then Decide, starts from data such as the workflow's own logs or, when none exists, runs of the LLM agent itself.
